# ruff: noqa
import os
import google.auth

_, project_id = google.auth.default()
os.environ["GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT"] = project_id or "vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0"
os.environ["GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION"] = "us-east4"
os.environ["GOOGLE_GENAI_USE_VERTEXAI"] = "True"

from google.adk.agents import Agent
from google.adk.apps import App

from app.registry import build_capabilities_summary
from app.tools import register_user, query_gmeet_agent, query_endpoint_agent, friction_score_agent, vpn_agent

_CAPABILITIES = build_capabilities_summary()

_INSTRUCTION = f"""You are the **EQES Main Assistant** — the central orchestrator for Verizon's
Employee Quality & Experience Services platform. You route user requests to the
appropriate specialized agent and relay their responses.

---

## AVAILABLE AGENTS

{_CAPABILITIES}

---

## CONVERSATION FLOW

### Step 1: Identify the User
When a user starts a conversation, collect their identity:
- Employee ID (EID), e.g. VZ12345
- Name
- Email

Then call `register_user` to save this for the session. Once registered, you
do NOT need to ask again — the identity persists across the conversation.

If the user has already identified themselves (e.g., "I'm John, VZ12345,
john.doe@verizon.com"), register them immediately and proceed to their question.

### Step 2: Route the Request
Analyze the user's question and determine which agent can handle it:

| Question topic | Tool to call |
|---|---|
| GMeet quality scores, meeting quality, audio/video metrics, score charts, employee name lookups, team/hierarchy/department GMeet scores, conference room scores, room comparisons, location room stats | `query_gmeet_agent` |
| Endpoint scores, endpoint health, device performance, machine/session/app breakdowns, team endpoint performance, fleet issues | `query_endpoint_agent` |
| Service desk friction scores, incident/interaction/hardware/software domain scores, resolution/handle times, bounce rates, fulfillment SLAs, day-1 scoring | `friction_score_agent` |
| VPN connectivity issues, VPN eQES health scores by EID/Machine, latency spikes, SAML/Duo lockouts, Time-in-Pain metrics (Hours in Frustration, Auth Blocks), local ISP vs corporate network bottlenecks, Gateway Churn | `vpn_agent` |

If the question doesn't match any available agent, say so clearly and list
what you CAN help with.

### Step 3: Relay the Response
- Pass the agent's full response to the user without truncating.
- If the agent returns charts or structured data, present them clearly.
- If the agent returned an error, explain what happened and suggest the user
  try again or rephrase.

---

## ROUTING RULES

- **One agent per question.** Don't call multiple agents for a single question.
- **Pass the full question.** Don't summarize or rewrite — the specialized agent
  needs the original context.
- **Don't fabricate answers.** If you're unsure which agent handles a topic,
  tell the user what agents are available instead of guessing.
- **EID forwarding.** The registered EID is automatically prepended to queries
  sent to remote agents, so users don't need to repeat it.

---

## RESPONSE STYLE

- Be concise and helpful.
- After routing a response, suggest related follow-up actions:
  > **What else can I help with?**
  > 1. [related query for the same agent]
  > 2. [query for a different available agent]
- If the user asks "what can you do?", list all available agents and their capabilities.
"""

root_agent = Agent(
    name="eqes_orchestrator",
    model="gemini-2.5-flash",
    instruction=_INSTRUCTION,
    tools=[
        register_user,
        query_gmeet_agent,
        query_endpoint_agent,
        friction_score_agent,
        vpn_agent,
    ],
)

app = App(
    root_agent=root_agent,
    name="app",
)

        return operations

        return self

def build_capabilities_summary() -> str:
    """Generate a markdown summary of all registered agents for the orchestrator instruction."""
    lines = []
    for key, entry in AGENT_REGISTRY.items():
        lines.append(f"### {entry.name} (`{key}`)")
        lines.append(f"{entry.description}")
        if entry.capabilities:
            for cap in entry.capabilities:
                lines.append(f"- {cap}")
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/app/remote_client.py | size=10136 | sha256=3ad835c4fef493eb3f236c2370584a19d1cb7a7e2385e95f5092eaba5e4eb868 =====
"""Generic client for querying remote agents deployed on Vertex AI Agent Runtime.

Uses the REST API :streamQuery endpoint directly and parses the response body
ourselves, bypassing the SDK's stream_query JSON parser which fails in deployed
containers with "Can only parse array of JSON objects, instead got {".
"""

import json
import logging
import threading
import time

import google.auth
import google.auth.transport.requests as google_auth_requests
import requests as http_requests
import vertexai
from vertexai import agent_engines

from app.config import GCP_PROJECT, GCP_REGION

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS = 3600  # 1 hour


def _extract_text_from_event(event: dict) -> str:
    """Extract text from a single ADK event dict, skipping function parts."""
    try:
        content = event.get("content")
        if not content or not isinstance(content, dict):
            logger.debug("Event has no valid content: %s", event)
            return ""

        parts = content.get("parts")
        if parts is None:
            logger.warning("Event content.parts is None: %s", event)
            return ""

        if not isinstance(parts, list):
            logger.warning("Event content.parts is not a list (type=%s): %s", type(parts), event)
            return ""

        texts = []
        for part in parts:
            if not isinstance(part, dict):
                continue
            if "functionCall" in part or "functionResponse" in part:
                continue
            if "function_call" in part or "function_response" in part:
                continue
            val = part.get("text")
            if val and isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
                texts.append(val.strip())

        return "\n".join(texts)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error extracting text from event: %s", exc)
        return ""


def _parse_stream_response(body: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Parse a streamQuery HTTP response body into a list of event dicts.

    Handles multiple formats:
    - JSON array: [{event1}, {event2}]
    - Single JSON object: {event1}
    - NDJSON: {event1}\\n{event2}
    - Comma-separated in array: [{event1},\\n{event2}\\n]
    """
    body = body.strip()
    if not body:
        return []

    # Format 1: JSON array
    if body.startswith("["):
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(body)
            if isinstance(parsed, list):
                return [e for e in parsed if isinstance(e, dict)]
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass

    # Format 2: single JSON object
    if body.startswith("{") and not "\n" in body:
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(body)
            if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                return [parsed]
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass

    # Format 3: NDJSON or comma-separated lines (with or without [ ] wrapper)
    events = []
    for line in body.split("\n"):
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line in ("[", "]", ","):
            continue
        if line.startswith("data:"):
            line = line[5:].strip()
        if line.endswith(","):
            line = line[:-1].strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            obj = json.loads(line)
            if isinstance(obj, dict):
                events.append(obj)
            elif isinstance(obj, list):
                events.extend(e for e in obj if isinstance(e, dict))
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue

    return events


class RemoteAgentClient:
    """Sends queries to a deployed Vertex AI agent and returns the text response.

    Primary: REST API :streamQuery with manual response parsing.
    Fallback: SDK stream_query (works locally).
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._sessions: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, float]] = {}
        self._remote_apps: dict[str, object] = {}
        self._credentials = None

    def _get_credentials(self):
        if self._credentials is None or not self._credentials.valid or self._credentials.expired:
            self._credentials, _ = google.auth.default(
                scopes=["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform"]
            )
        if not self._credentials.valid or self._credentials.expired:
            self._credentials.refresh(google_auth_requests.Request())
        return self._credentials

    def _get_remote_app(self, resource_name: str):
        if resource_name not in self._remote_apps:
            vertexai.init(project=GCP_PROJECT, location=GCP_REGION)
            self._remote_apps[resource_name] = agent_engines.get(resource_name)
        return self._remote_apps[resource_name]

    def _get_or_create_session(
        self, remote_app, resource_name: str, user_id: str
    ) -> str:
        key = (resource_name, user_id)
        now = time.time()

        with self._lock:
            if key in self._sessions:
                session_id, created = self._sessions[key]
                if now - created < _SESSION_TTL_SECONDS:
                    return session_id
                logger.info("Session expired for %s / %s, creating new one", resource_name, user_id)

        session = remote_app.create_session(user_id=user_id)
        session_id = session.id if hasattr(session, "id") else session["id"]

        with self._lock:
            self._sessions[key] = (session_id, now)

        return session_id

    def query(self, resource_name: str, user_id: str, message: str) -> str:
        """Send a message to a remote agent and return the text response."""
        remote_app = self._get_remote_app(resource_name)
        session_id = self._get_or_create_session(remote_app, resource_name, user_id)

        logger.info(
            "Querying remote agent %s (session=%s, user=%s)",
            resource_name, session_id, user_id,
        )

        # Primary: REST API :streamQuery with manual parsing
        try:
            text = self._query_stream_rest(resource_name, user_id, session_id, message)
            if text:
                logger.info("REST streamQuery returned %d chars", len(text))
                return text
            logger.warning("REST streamQuery returned no text")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("REST streamQuery failed: %s, trying SDK fallback", exc)

        # Fallback: SDK stream_query (works locally)
        return self._query_stream_sdk(remote_app, user_id, session_id, message)

    def _query_stream_rest(
        self, resource_name: str, user_id: str, session_id: str, message: str
    ) -> str:
        """Query via REST API :streamQuery endpoint with manual response parsing."""
        credentials = self._get_credentials()

        url = (
            f"https://{GCP_REGION}-aiplatform.googleapis.com"
            f"/v1beta1/{resource_name}:streamQuery"
        )

        headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {credentials.token}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
        }

        payload = {
            "input": {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "session_id": session_id,
                "message": message,
            }
        }

        logger.info("REST streamQuery to %s", url)
        resp = http_requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=120)

        if resp.status_code != 200:
            logger.warning(
                "REST streamQuery HTTP %d: %s", resp.status_code, resp.text[:500]
            )
            resp.raise_for_status()

        body = resp.text
        logger.info("REST streamQuery response length: %d chars", len(body))
        logger.info("REST streamQuery response start: %.200s", body[:200])

        events = _parse_stream_response(body)
        logger.info("Parsed %d events from response", len(events))

        # Find the last event with actual text (the agent's final response)
        for event in reversed(events):
            text = _extract_text_from_event(event)
            if text:
                return text

        # If structured extraction failed, log all events for debugging
        if events:
            logger.warning(
                "No text found in %d events. First event keys: %s",
                len(events),
                list(events[0].keys()) if events else "none",
            )

        return ""

    def _query_stream_sdk(
        self, remote_app, user_id: str, session_id: str, message: str
    ) -> str:
        """Fallback: query via SDK stream_query (works locally)."""
        text_parts: list[str] = []

        try:
            for event in remote_app.stream_query(
                user_id=user_id,
                session_id=session_id,
                message=message,
            ):
                if isinstance(event, dict):
                    text = _extract_text_from_event(event)
                else:
                    text = ""
                    try:
                        content = getattr(event, "content", None)
                        if content and hasattr(content, "parts"):
                            parts = content.parts
                            if parts is not None:
                                for part in parts:
                                    val = getattr(part, "text", None)
                                    if val and isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
                                        text = val.strip()
                            else:
                                logger.warning("SDK event content.parts is None")
                    except Exception as part_exc:
                        logger.warning("Error extracting text from SDK event: %s", part_exc)

                if text:
                    text_parts.append(text)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("SDK stream_query error after %d parts: %s", len(text_parts), exc)

        if text_parts:
            return "\n".join(text_parts).strip()

        return "The remote agent processed the request but the response could not be read. Please try again."


remote_client = RemoteAgentClient()

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/app/response_validator.py | size=5060 | sha256=2eabcf6f21d7dba6abe07439827e80e35febf2c31c2bf0ba2ff41c19fd05a812 =====
"""Response validator to prevent 'NoneType' object is not subscriptable errors.

This module adds defensive checks for ADK event responses where parts can be None.
"""
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def validate_event_parts(event: Any) -> bool:
    """Validate that an event has properly structured parts.

    Args:
        event: ADK event object or dict

    Returns:
        True if event has valid parts, False otherwise
    """
    try:
        # Handle dict-style events
        if isinstance(event, dict):
            content = event.get("content")
            if not content:
                return False

            if not isinstance(content, dict):
                logger.warning("Event content is not a dict: %s", type(content))
                return False

            parts = content.get("parts")
            if parts is None:
                logger.warning("Event content.parts is None")
                return False

            if not isinstance(parts, list):
                logger.warning("Event content.parts is not a list: %s", type(parts))
                return False

            return True

        # Handle object-style events
        content = getattr(event, "content", None)
        if not content:
            return False

        parts = getattr(content, "parts", None)
        if parts is None:
            logger.warning("Event content.parts is None (object)")
            return False

        return True

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error validating event parts: %s", exc)
        return False


def extract_text_from_event(event: Any) -> str:
    """Safely extract text from an ADK event.

    Args:
        event: ADK event object or dict

    Returns:
        Extracted text, or empty string if extraction fails
    """
    try:
        # Handle dict-style events
        if isinstance(event, dict):
            content = event.get("content")
            if not content or not isinstance(content, dict):
                return ""

            parts = content.get("parts")
            if parts is None:
                logger.warning("Event content.parts is None, skipping text extraction")
                return ""

            if not isinstance(parts, list):
                logger.warning("Event content.parts is not a list: %s", type(parts))
                return ""

            texts = []
            for part in parts:
                if not isinstance(part, dict):
                    continue

                # Skip function calls/responses
                if any(key in part for key in ["functionCall", "functionResponse",
                                               "function_call", "function_response"]):
                    continue

                val = part.get("text")
                if val and isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
                    texts.append(val.strip())

            return "\n".join(texts)

        # Handle object-style events
        content = getattr(event, "content", None)
        if not content:
            return ""

        parts = getattr(content, "parts", None)
        if parts is None:
            logger.warning("Event content.parts is None (object), skipping text extraction")
            return ""

        texts = []
        try:
            for part in parts:
                # Skip function calls/responses
                if hasattr(part, "function_call") or hasattr(part, "function_response"):
                    continue

                val = getattr(part, "text", None)
                if val and isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
                    texts.append(val.strip())
        except TypeError as exc:
            logger.warning("Error iterating parts (not iterable): %s", exc)
            return ""

        return "\n".join(texts)

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error extracting text from event: %s", exc)
        return ""


def sanitize_event(event: Any) -> Any:
    """Sanitize an event to ensure it has valid parts structure.

    Args:
        event: ADK event object or dict

    Returns:
        Sanitized event with parts guaranteed to be a list
    """
    try:
        # Handle dict-style events
        if isinstance(event, dict):
            if "content" in event and isinstance(event["content"], dict):
                if "parts" not in event["content"] or event["content"]["parts"] is None:
                    logger.warning("Sanitizing event: setting parts to empty list")
                    event["content"]["parts"] = []
            return event

        # Handle object-style events (harder to modify, just log)
        content = getattr(event, "content", None)
        if content:
            parts = getattr(content, "parts", None)
            if parts is None:
                logger.warning("Event has None parts but cannot modify object-style event")

        return event

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error sanitizing event: %s", exc)
        return event

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/app/tool_wrapper.py | size=2451 | sha256=416088f3ec0b6553789cb5102cf15916eedb2e72cae6d586650312c296ccf48e =====
"""Tool wrapper to add defensive error handling to all tool functions."""
import functools
import logging
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def safe_tool(func: Callable) -> Callable:
    """Decorator to wrap tool functions with error handling.

    Ensures tool functions always return valid dict responses and never
    cause the agent to crash.

    Args:
        func: Tool function to wrap

    Returns:
        Wrapped function with error handling
    """
    @functools.wraps(func)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            logger.info(f"Calling tool: {func.__name__}")
            result = func(*args, **kwargs)

            # Ensure result is a dict
            if not isinstance(result, dict):
                logger.warning(
                    f"Tool {func.__name__} returned non-dict: {type(result)}"
                )
                return {
                    "status": "error",
                    "error": f"Tool {func.__name__} returned invalid response type",
                    "result": str(result),
                }

            logger.info(f"Tool {func.__name__} completed successfully")
            return result

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception(f"Tool {func.__name__} failed: %s", exc)
            return {
                "status": "error",
                "error": f"Tool {func.__name__} encountered an error: {str(exc)}",
                "function": func.__name__,
            }

    return wrapper


def validate_tool_response(response: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate and sanitize a tool response.

    Args:
        response: Raw tool response

    Returns:
        Validated dict response
    """
    if response is None:
        logger.warning("Tool returned None, converting to error response")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "error": "Tool returned None instead of a valid response",
        }

    if not isinstance(response, dict):
        logger.warning(f"Tool returned non-dict: {type(response)}")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "error": f"Invalid response type: {type(response)}",
            "raw_response": str(response),
        }

    # Ensure status field exists
    if "status" not in response:
        logger.debug("Adding missing 'status' field to tool response")
        response["status"] = "success"

    return response

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/app/tools.py | size=6554 | sha256=c8e0616cb70659772e01816461e865a78081b248cdde615f54c9270feb3fa096 =====
"""Tool functions for the EQES orchestrator agent.

Each remote agent gets one tool function. To add a new agent:
  1. Add its AgentEntry in registry.py
  2. Add a tool function here following the pattern below
  3. Import and register it in agent.py
"""

import logging

from google.adk.tools import ToolContext

from app.registry import AGENT_REGISTRY
from app.remote_client import remote_client

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _query_remote_agent(
    agent_key: str, question: str, tool_context: ToolContext
) -> dict:
    """Shared helper that routes a question to a registered remote agent."""
    entry = AGENT_REGISTRY.get(agent_key)
    if not entry:
        return {"status": "error", "message": f"Unknown agent: {agent_key}"}

    user_id = tool_context.state.get("user_id", "anonymous")
    eid = tool_context.state.get("eid")

    message = question
    if eid:
        message = f"[User EID: {eid}] {question}"

    try:
        response = remote_client.query(
            resource_name=entry.resource_name,
            user_id=user_id,
            message=message,
        )

        # Handle empty or None responses
        if not response or not response.strip():
            logger.warning("Empty response from remote agent %s", agent_key)
            return {
                "status": "error",
                "agent": entry.name,
                "message": f"{entry.name} returned an empty response. The agent may have encountered an internal error. Please try again.",
            }

        return {"status": "success", "agent": entry.name, "response": response}
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error querying remote agent %s", agent_key)
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "agent": entry.name,
            "message": f"Failed to reach {entry.name}: {exc}",
        }


# ── Tool: register user identity ────────────────────────────────────────────────


def register_user(
    eid: str,
    name: str,
    email: str,
    tool_context: ToolContext,
) -> dict:
    """Register the user's identity for the session.

    Args:
        eid: Employee ID (e.g. VZ12345).
        name: Employee full name.
        email: Employee email address.
        tool_context: ADK tool context (injected automatically).

    Returns:
        Confirmation of registered identity.
    """
    tool_context.state["eid"] = eid.strip()
    tool_context.state["user_name"] = name.strip()
    tool_context.state["user_email"] = email.strip()
    tool_context.state["user_id"] = eid.strip()
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "message": f"Registered user {name} (EID: {eid}, email: {email}). "
        "All subsequent queries will use this identity.",
    }

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/check_deployment_status.py | size=2359 | sha256=912ccfc31edf0a5cdd7ef3fc55f4cbb63f7382dfbc70a0935604f99d4ce5da4c =====
"""Check the deployment status of the eqesmainagent reasoning engine."""
import time
import vertexai
from vertexai import agent_engines

PROJECT_ID = "vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0"
REGION = "us-east4"
RESOURCE_NAME = "projects/1041813482705/locations/us-east4/reasoningEngines/7058204943345254400"

def check_status():
    """Check and wait for the reasoning engine to become ACTIVE."""
    vertexai.init(project=PROJECT_ID, location=REGION)

    print(f"Checking status of: {RESOURCE_NAME}")
    print("-" * 80)

    max_wait = 600  # 10 minutes
    check_interval = 10  # Check every 10 seconds
    elapsed = 0

    while elapsed < max_wait:
        try:
            engine = agent_engines.get(RESOURCE_NAME)

            # Try to get state attribute
            state = getattr(engine, 'state', 'UNKNOWN')
            display_name = getattr(engine, 'display_name', 'Unknown')

            print(f"[{elapsed}s] Agent: {display_name}")
            print(f"[{elapsed}s] State: {state}")

            if state == "ACTIVE" or state == 1:  # 1 is the enum value for ACTIVE
                print("\n✅ Agent is ACTIVE and ready for deployment!")
                return True
            elif state == "UPDATING" or state == 2:  # 2 is UPDATING
                print(f"[{elapsed}s] Still updating... waiting {check_interval}s")
            elif state == "FAILED" or state == 4:  # 4 is FAILED
                print("\n❌ Agent deployment FAILED!")
                return False
            else:
                print(f"[{elapsed}s] Unknown state: {state}")

        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[{elapsed}s] Error checking status: {exc}")
            print(f"[{elapsed}s] Retrying in {check_interval}s...")

        time.sleep(check_interval)
        elapsed += check_interval

    print(f"\n⏱️ Timeout after {max_wait}s. Agent may still be updating.")
    return False


if __name__ == "__main__":
    success = check_status()

    if success:
        print("\n" + "=" * 80)
        print("You can now run the deployment:")
        print("  python deploy_to_agent_runtime.py")
        print("=" * 80)
    else:
        print("\n" + "=" * 80)
        print("Wait a bit longer or check the GCP console for errors:")
        print(f"  https://console.cloud.google.com/vertex-ai/agent-builder/engines?project={PROJECT_ID}")
        print("=" * 80)
