===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/config.py | size=6657 | sha256=fd546c4f458931e04834279af303d86c1fe2e82b99b616df6e91b30bc49e95ac =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

try:
    from dotenv import load_dotenv
    load_dotenv(Path(__file__).parent.parent / ".env", override=False)
except ImportError:
    pass

# Config file is always relative to this file's package root
_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "agent_runtime_config.json"


def _load_json() -> dict:
    if not _CONFIG_PATH.is_file():
        logging.getLogger(__name__).warning(
            "Config file not found at '%s'; using runtime-safe defaults.",
            _CONFIG_PATH,
        )
        return {}

    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
        return json.load(f)


@dataclass
class AgentConfig:
    """Per-agent deployment settings."""
    key: str
    display_name: str
    description: str
    min_instances: int
    max_instances: int
    otel_service_name: str
    entrypoint_module: str
    entrypoint_object: str
    cpu: str = "4"
    memory: str = "8Gi"
    container_concurrency: int = 9
    num_workers: int = 1


@dataclass
class MemoryBankConfig:
    """
    Memory Bank strategy configuration.

    mode:
      'disabled'      — no long-term memory (default).
      'decoupled'     — separate Memory Bank resource managed outside Agent Runtime
                        (requires MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME env var and a pre-created
                        resource via deploy/create_memory_bank.py).
      'agent_runtime' — built-in Memory Bank managed by Agent Runtime / AdkApp
                        (requires AGENT_ENGINE_RESOURCE_NAME or the runtime sets it;
                        no pre-created resource needed).
    """
    mode: str = "disabled"           # disabled | decoupled | agent_runtime
    idle_trigger_seconds: int = 300  # idle-duration trigger for memory consolidation
    top_k: int = 5                   # max memories to retrieve per query


@dataclass
class DeployConfig:
    """Top-level deployment configuration (JSON + env overrides."""
    project_id: str
    project_number: str
    location: str
    agent_framework: str
    identity_type: str
    api_version: str
    psc_network_attachment: str
    container_image: str = ""
    log_level: str = "INFO"
    labels: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    dns_peering_configs: list = field(default_factory=list)
    agents: dict[str, AgentConfig] = field(default_factory=dict)
    memory_bank: MemoryBankConfig = field(default_factory=MemoryBankConfig)

    def resource_name(self, resource_id: str) -> str:
        return (
            f"projects/{self.project_id}/locations/{self.location}"
            f"/reasoningEngines/{resource_id}"
        )

    def endpoint_url(self, resource_id: str) -> str:
        return (
            f"https://{self.location}-aiplatform.googleapis.com/{self.api_version}/"
            f"projects/{self.project_id}/locations/{self.location}/"
            f"reasoningEngines/{resource_id}"
        )


def _build_config() -> DeployConfig:
    raw: dict[str, Any] = _load_json()

    agents = {
        key: AgentConfig(
            key=key,
            display_name=val["display_name"],
            description=val["description"],
            min_instances=int(val["min_instances"]),
            max_instances=int(val["max_instances"]),
            otel_service_name=val["otel_service_name"],
            entrypoint_module=val["entrypoint_module"],
            entrypoint_object=val["entrypoint_object"],
            cpu=val.get("cpu", "1"),
            memory=val.get("memory", "2Gi"),
            container_concurrency=int(val.get("container_concurrency", 2)),
            num_workers=int(val.get("num_workers", 2)),
        )
        for key, val in raw.get("agents", {}).items()
    }

    mb_raw = raw.get("memory_bank", {})
    # env var MEMORY_BANK_MODE overrides config file (useful in CI / local dev)
    mb_mode = (
        os.environ.get("MEMORY_BANK_MODE", "").strip().lower()
        or mb_raw.get("mode", "disabled")
    )
    memory_bank = MemoryBankConfig(
        mode=mb_mode,
        idle_trigger_seconds=int(mb_raw.get("idle_trigger_seconds", 300)),
        top_k=int(mb_raw.get("top_k", 5)),
    )

    return DeployConfig(
        project_id=os.environ.get("GCP_PROJECT_ID", ""),
        project_number=os.environ.get("GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER", ""),
        location=raw.get("location", os.environ.get("GCP_LOCATION", "us-east4")),
        agent_framework=raw.get("agent_framework", "google-adk"),
        identity_type=raw.get("identity_type", "AGENT_IDENTITY"),
        api_version=raw.get("api_version", os.environ.get("API_VERSION", "v1")),
        container_image=raw.get("container_image", ""),
        log_level=raw.get("log_level", os.environ.get("LOG_LEVEL", "INFO")),
        labels=raw.get("labels", {}),
        psc_network_attachment=raw.get("psc_network_attachment", ""),
        dns_peering_configs=raw.get("dns_peering_configs", []),
        agents=agents,
        memory_bank=memory_bank,
    )


# Module-level singleton
cfg: DeployConfig = _build_config()


def agent_cfg(key: str) -> AgentConfig:
    """Return per-agent config by key (e.g. 'research')."""
    try:
        return cfg.agents[key]
    except KeyError:
        raise KeyError(
            f"No agent config for key '{key}'. "
            f"Available: {list(cfg.agents.keys())}"
        )


def vertexai_client():
    """Return a Vertex AI client pre-configured for this project/location.

    Respects REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE / SSL_CERT_FILE for corporate CA bundles.
    Set PYTHONHTTPSVERIFY=0 to disable TLS verification in dev/test only.
    """
    import httpx
    import vertexai

    ca_bundle = os.environ.get("REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE") or os.environ.get("SSL_CERT_FILE")
    if ca_bundle and not os.path.isfile(ca_bundle):
        import logging as _log
        _log.warning(
            f"[vertexai_client] CA bundle not found at '{ca_bundle}', "
            "falling back to system trust store."
        )
        ca_bundle = None

    if os.environ.get("PYTHONHTTPSVERIFY") == "0":
        verify: bool | str = False
    elif ca_bundle:
        verify = ca_bundle
    else:
        verify = True

    return vertexai.Client(
        project=cfg.project_id,
        location=cfg.location,
        http_options={
            "api_version": cfg.api_version,
            "httpx_client": httpx.Client(verify=verify),
        },
    )

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/deploy_utils.py | size=4000 | sha256=54e8dad424658c9204fc94c1f5c3cac039276075eeb52e4dbaf46b989e087b88 =====
"""
deploy_utils.py — Reusable helpers for Agent Runtime deployments.

Keeps deploy scripts thin: all shared logic lives here.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import os
import re
import sys

from vertexai._genai import _agent_engines_utils


def _merge_no_proxy_hosts(raw_no_proxy: str, required_hosts: list[str]) -> str:
    hosts = [host.strip() for host in raw_no_proxy.split(",") if host.strip()]
    for required in required_hosts:
        if required not in hosts:
            hosts.append(required)
    return ",".join(hosts)


def _parse_hosts(raw_hosts: str) -> list[str]:
    return [host.strip() for host in raw_hosts.split(",") if host.strip()]


def load_agent_instance(entrypoint_module: str, entrypoint_object: str):
    """Import and return the agent entrypoint object."""
    module = importlib.import_module(entrypoint_module)
    return getattr(module, entrypoint_object)


def generate_class_methods(agent_instance) -> list:
    """Derive the class_methods spec from the agent's register_operations()."""
    registered = _agent_engines_utils._get_registered_operations(agent=agent_instance)
    spec = _agent_engines_utils._generate_class_methods_spec_or_raise(
        agent=agent_instance, operations=registered
    )
    return [_agent_engines_utils._to_dict(m) for m in spec]


def build_proxy_env_vars() -> dict:
    """Return proxy env-var dict read entirely from the environment (no hardcoding).

    Expected .env / environment variables:
        PROXY_SERVER_URL   e.g. http://swp.agentengine.internal:8888
        NO_PROXY           comma-separated no-proxy hosts
        REQUIRED_NO_PROXY_HOSTS  comma-separated hosts that must always bypass proxy
    """
    proxy = os.environ.get("PROXY_SERVER_URL", "")
    no_proxy_raw = os.environ.get("NO_PROXY", "")
    required_no_proxy_raw = os.environ.get("REQUIRED_NO_PROXY_HOSTS", "")
    no_proxy = _merge_no_proxy_hosts(no_proxy_raw, _parse_hosts(required_no_proxy_raw))
    if not proxy:
        return {}
    return {
        "PROXY_SERVER_URL": proxy,
        "PYVEGAS_PROXY_URL": proxy,
        "HTTP_PROXY": proxy,
        "HTTPS_PROXY": proxy,
        "http_proxy": proxy,
        "https_proxy": proxy,
        "NO_PROXY": no_proxy,
        "no_proxy": no_proxy,
    }


_LABEL_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$")


def validate_labels(labels: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    """Validate labels against Agent Runtime constraints.

    Rules: max 64 labels, keys/values must match ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$,
    keys < 64 chars, values < 64 chars (both < 128 bytes UTF-8).
    Calls sys.exit with a clear message on any violation.
    """
    if len(labels) > 64:
        sys.exit("Invalid labels: no more than 64 labels are allowed.")

    errors: list[str] = []
    for key, value in labels.items():
        key_str, value_str = str(key), str(value)
        if len(key_str) >= 64 or len(key_str.encode("utf-8")) >= 128:
            errors.append(f"key '{key_str}': key too long (max 63 chars)")
        if len(value_str) >= 64 or len(value_str.encode("utf-8")) >= 128:
            errors.append(f"key '{key_str}': value too long (max 63 chars)")
        if not _LABEL_PATTERN.fullmatch(key_str):
            errors.append(f"key '{key_str}': must match ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$")
        if value_str and not _LABEL_PATTERN.fullmatch(value_str):
            errors.append(f"value for '{key_str}' ('{value_str}'): must match ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$")

    if errors:
        sys.exit("Invalid labels in config/agent_runtime_config.json:\n  - " + "\n  - ".join(errors))


def validate_requirements_file(project_root: str, filename: str) -> str:
    """Verify the requirements file exists; exit with a clear message otherwise."""
    path = os.path.join(project_root, filename)
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        sys.exit(f"Requirements file not found: {path}")
    return filename

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/memory/__init__.py | size=2292 | sha256=6929207bb186e8fe1235d042112a8a5cac707fc85e55b3cc316c3dc6fa2c2d6a =====
"""
common/memory — Pluggable long-term memory for ADK agents.

Three strategies, selected via ``memory_bank.mode`` in
``config/agent_runtime_config.json`` or the ``MEMORY_BANK_MODE`` env var:

  disabled       — No long-term memory.  Zero cloud dependencies.
                   Safe for local dev and unit tests.

  decoupled      — Standalone Vertex AI Memory Bank resource, managed
                   independently of Agent Runtime.  Persists across
                   redeployments; shareable across multiple agents.
                   Requires ``MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME`` env var and a
                   pre-created resource (``deploy/create_memory_bank.py``).

  agent_runtime  — Built-in ADK ``VertexAiMemoryBankService``, managed by
                   Agent Runtime / AdkApp.  Zero ops — pass
                   ``svc.adk_memory_service`` to ``App()`` and ADK handles
                   everything.  No pre-created resource needed.

Typical usage
-------------
    from common.memory import create_memory_service

    # Reads mode from config/env automatically
    memory = create_memory_service()

    # Wire into ADK App (None-safe for non-agent_runtime modes)
    app = App(..., memory_service=memory.adk_memory_service)

    # In request handler (decoupled mode only — no-op in other modes)
    import asyncio
    asyncio.create_task(
        asyncio.to_thread(memory.ingest_turn_sync, user_id, session_id, text)
    )

    # Optional manual prompt augmentation (decoupled mode only)
    enriched = memory.build_context_prompt(user_query, user_id)

Public API
----------
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from common.memory.base import BaseMemoryService, MemoryMode
from common.memory.null import NullMemoryService
from common.memory.decoupled import DecoupledMemoryService
from common.memory.agent_runtime import AgentRuntimeMemoryService
from common.memory.factory import create_memory_service

__all__ = [
    # Core interface & constants
    "BaseMemoryService",
    "MemoryMode",
    # Concrete strategies (for type hints / isinstance checks / DI)
    "NullMemoryService",
    "DecoupledMemoryService",
    "AgentRuntimeMemoryService",
    # Primary entry point
    "create_memory_service",
]

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/memory/agent_runtime.py | size=10393 | sha256=7808ac0bf1d29f1856de873c2e821b114ea06b11ea8ba44100095904e2a41c6a =====
"""
common/memory/agent_runtime.py � Built-in ADK memory strategy (MemoryMode.AGENT_RUNTIME).

Long-term memory backed by ADK's ``VertexAiMemoryBankService``, which uses
the agent's own Reasoning Engine resource as the memory store.

In BYOC deployments (custom container / FastAPI), the ADK runner is NOT active,
so memory ingestion and retrieval are handled manually � the same pattern as
DecoupledMemoryService, but using the ADK-native service object instead of the
raw Vertex AI client.

Required env vars:
  GCP_PROJECT_ID            � GCP project ID.
  GCP_LOCATION              � GCP region (default: us-central1).
  AGENT_ENGINE_RESOURCE_ID  � Numeric ID of the deployed agent's Reasoning Engine,
                               e.g. "8945776137166913536".
                               Available after first deployment.  Set this in .env
                               and redeploy to enable long-term memory.

How memory_service differs from App():
  google.adk.apps.App has extra="forbid" and declares NO memory_service field in
  the installed ADK version.  Do NOT pass memory_service to App().  In managed
  (non-BYOC) deployments the ADK runner wires memory separately; in BYOC we
  handle ingest/retrieve explicitly in main.py.

Ingestion  (called via asyncio.to_thread in main.py \u2014 safe, no event loop in thread):
  ingest_turn_sync \u2192 asyncio.run(add_memory(...))

Retrieval  (called via asyncio.to_thread from main.py \u2014 safe in thread):
  build_context_prompt \u2192 asyncio.run(search_memory(...))
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from common.memory.base import BaseMemoryService, MemoryMode

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Back-reference phrases that signal the user wants long-term context even
# when a short-term session already exists (same policy as decoupled mode).
_BACK_REF_PHRASES = (
    "as i said", "as mentioned", "earlier", "previous", "before",
    "last time", "you know my", "my preference", "my constraints",
    "based on what i told", "what did i say", "remind me",
)


class AgentRuntimeMemoryService(BaseMemoryService):
    """
    Long-term memory via ADK's ``VertexAiMemoryBankService``.

    The Reasoning Engine resource that runs the agent also serves as its Memory
    Bank.  ``AGENT_ENGINE_RESOURCE_ID`` must be set to the agent's own resource
    ID (available after first deployment).

    In BYOC mode, this class performs manual ingest/retrieve because the ADK
    runner is not active.  All async ADK methods are wrapped with
    ``asyncio.run()`` and must be called from a thread (via
    ``asyncio.to_thread``), never directly from the async event loop.

    When to use
    -----------
    - ``memory_bank.mode = "agent_runtime"`` in agent_runtime_config.json.
    - Zero separate infrastructure: the running agent IS the memory bank.
    - Acceptable that memory is tied to this agent's deployment lifecycle.

    Args:
        app_name  : ADK app name used as memory scope (default: "research_agent2").
        project   : GCP project ID (falls back to ``GCP_PROJECT_ID``).
        location  : GCP region (falls back to ``GCP_LOCATION``).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        app_name: str = "research_agent2",
        project: Optional[str] = None,
        location: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.project = project or os.environ.get("GCP_PROJECT_ID")
        self.location = location or os.environ.get("GCP_LOCATION", "us-central1")
        self._app_name = app_name
        self._initialized = False
        self.adk_memory_service: Optional[Any] = None
        self._init_service()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initialisation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _init_service(self) -> None:
        """
        Instantiate VertexAiMemoryBankService.

        Agent Runtime automatically injects ``GOOGLE_CLOUD_AGENT_ENGINE_ID``
        into the container environment — no manual configuration needed.
        See: https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-agent-platform/scale/memory-bank/setup
        """
        # Agent Runtime injects this automatically — no deploy-time patching needed.
        agent_engine_id = (
            os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_AGENT_ENGINE_ID", "")
            or os.environ.get("AGENT_ENGINE_RESOURCE_ID", "")  # local dev fallback
        ).strip()
        if not agent_engine_id:
            logger.warning(
                "[AgentRuntimeMemory] GOOGLE_CLOUD_AGENT_ENGINE_ID not set — "
                "this is injected automatically by Agent Runtime. "
                "For local dev, set AGENT_ENGINE_RESOURCE_ID manually. "
                "Long-term memory is disabled."
            )
            return

        try:
            from google.adk.memory import VertexAiMemoryBankService
            self.adk_memory_service = VertexAiMemoryBankService(
                project=self.project,
                location=self.location,
                agent_engine_id=agent_engine_id,
            )
            self._initialized = True
            logger.info(
                "[AgentRuntimeMemory] Ready | agent_engine_id=%s app=%s",
                agent_engine_id, self._app_name,
            )
        except ImportError:
            logger.error(
                "[AgentRuntimeMemory] google.adk.memory.VertexAiMemoryBankService "
                "not found. Long-term memory is disabled."
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(
                "[AgentRuntimeMemory] Failed to initialise "
                "VertexAiMemoryBankService: %s. Long-term memory is disabled.", exc,
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # BaseMemoryService interface
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def mode(self) -> str:
        return MemoryMode.AGENT_RUNTIME

    @property
    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return self._initialized and self.adk_memory_service is not None

    def ingest_turn_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: str,
        text: str,
        event_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Ingest a user turn into memory (blocking).

        MUST be called via ``asyncio.to_thread()`` \u2014 uses ``asyncio.run()``
        internally, which requires a thread without a running event loop.
        """
        if not self.is_available or not user_id or not text:
            return
        try:
            from google.adk.memory.memory_entry import MemoryEntry
            from google.genai import types as genai_types

            entry = MemoryEntry(
                author="user",
                content=genai_types.Content(
                    parts=[genai_types.Part(text=text)],
                    role="user",
                ),
            )
            asyncio.run(
                self.adk_memory_service.add_memory(
                    app_name=self._app_name,
                    user_id=user_id,
                    memories=[entry],
                )
            )
            logger.debug(
                "[AgentRuntimeMemory] Ingested | user=%s session=%s",
                user_id, session_id,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("[AgentRuntimeMemory] ingest_turn_sync failed: %s", exc)

    def should_consult_memory(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: Optional[str],
        query_text: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Session-first retrieval policy (same as DecoupledMemoryService).

        - No active session \u2192 always consult long-term memory.
        - Active session + back-reference phrase \u2192 consult long-term memory.
        - Active session + fresh query \u2192 rely on session context only.
        """
        if not self.is_available or not user_id:
            return False

        query = (query_text or "").strip().lower()
        if not query:
            return False

        if not session_id:
            return True

        return any(phrase in query for phrase in _BACK_REF_PHRASES)

    def retrieve_memories_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
        top_k: int = 5,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        No-op in direct sync calls \u2014 use ``build_context_prompt`` instead.

        ``VertexAiMemoryBankService.search_memory`` is async.  Retrieval is done
        inside ``build_context_prompt`` via ``asyncio.run()``, which requires
        being called from a thread (via ``asyncio.to_thread``).
        """
        return []

    def build_context_prompt(
        self,
        user_query: str,
        user_id: str,
        include_memories: bool = True,
        top_k: int = 3,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Retrieve memories and prepend to user_query (blocking).

        MUST be called via ``asyncio.to_thread()`` \u2014 uses ``asyncio.run()``
        internally, which requires a thread without a running event loop.

        In main.py both ingest and retrieval are already wrapped in
        ``asyncio.to_thread``, so this is safe.
        """
        if not include_memories or not self.is_available:
            return user_query

        try:
            response = asyncio.run(
                self.adk_memory_service.search_memory(
                    app_name=self._app_name,
                    user_id=user_id,
                    query=user_query,
                )
            )
            memories = [
                m for m in (response.memories or [])[:top_k]
                if m.content and m.content.parts and m.content.parts[0].text
            ]
            if not memories:
                return user_query

            header = "## Your Previous Context\n" + "".join(
                f"- {m.content.parts[0].text}\n" for m in memories
            )
            logger.info(
                "[AgentRuntimeMemory] Injected %d memories for user=%s",
                len(memories), user_id,
            )
            return f"{header}\n## Current Question\n{user_query}"
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("[AgentRuntimeMemory] build_context_prompt failed: %s", exc)
            return user_query

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/memory/base.py | size=5791 | sha256=96d9e7db5406fde4d5973056299f0ef0ac8252f4fa53b2587a1dbe2fbc2bd013 =====
"""
common/memory/base.py — Abstract base class and mode constants.

Any agent in this project can depend on these types without pulling in
any Vertex AI or ADK SDK imports.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Mode constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class MemoryMode:
    """
    Supported memory-backend modes.

    Set via ``memory_bank.mode`` in agent_runtime_config.json
    or the ``MEMORY_BANK_MODE`` environment variable.
    """
    DISABLED      = "disabled"       # no-op; zero dependencies
    DECOUPLED     = "decoupled"      # standalone Memory Bank resource (pre-created)
    AGENT_RUNTIME = "agent_runtime"  # built-in ADK VertexAiMemoryBankService


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Abstract base
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class BaseMemoryService(ABC):
    """
    Common interface for all long-term memory strategies.

    Implementations
    ---------------
    NullMemoryService         — MemoryMode.DISABLED
    DecoupledMemoryService    — MemoryMode.DECOUPLED
    AgentRuntimeMemoryService — MemoryMode.AGENT_RUNTIME

    The interface is intentionally thin so that:
    - Agent code never needs ``if mode == ...`` branches.
    - Strategies can be swapped via config with zero agent-code changes.
    - Unit tests can inject NullMemoryService without any cloud credentials.

    ADK wiring
    ----------
    When mode is ``agent_runtime``, set ``App(memory_service=svc.adk_memory_service)``
    in your agent builder.  For all other modes ``adk_memory_service`` is ``None``.
    """

    #: Expose the ADK VertexAiMemoryBankService instance for App() wiring.
    #: Only populated by AgentRuntimeMemoryService; None for all other modes.
    adk_memory_service: Optional[Any] = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Core interface — all strategies must implement these
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def mode(self) -> str:
        """Return the MemoryMode string for this instance."""

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        """True when the backend is reachable and ready."""

    @abstractmethod
    def ingest_turn_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: str,
        text: str,
        event_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Ingest a single user turn into long-term memory (synchronous / blocking).

        **Always** call this via ``asyncio.to_thread()`` to avoid blocking the
        event loop.  Fire-and-forget via ``asyncio.create_task`` is fine.

        Args:
            user_id    : Scopes memories to a user; required.
            session_id : Stream ID for isolation between sessions.
            text       : User message text.
            event_id   : Optional deduplication ID; auto-generated if omitted.
        """

    @abstractmethod
    def should_consult_memory(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: Optional[str],
        query_text: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when long-term memory retrieval is warranted.

        Implementations apply a retrieval policy suited to their mode, e.g.:
        - NullMemoryService    → always False
        - DecoupledMemoryService → session-first heuristic
        - AgentRuntimeMemoryService → always False (ADK injects automatically)
        """

    @abstractmethod
    def retrieve_memories_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
        top_k: int = 5,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Retrieve relevant memories for *query* (synchronous / blocking).

        Returns a list of dicts with at minimum a ``"fact"`` key.
        Returns ``[]`` when unavailable or no memories exist yet.

        Args:
            user_id : User identifier.
            query   : Search query text (typically the user's current message).
            top_k   : Maximum number of memories to return.
        """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Shared helper (override in strategies if behaviour differs)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def build_context_prompt(
        self,
        user_query: str,
        user_id: str,
        include_memories: bool = True,
        top_k: int = 3,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Prepend retrieved long-term memories to *user_query* (synchronous).

        Returns the original query unchanged when:
        - ``include_memories`` is False, or
        - the backend is unavailable, or
        - no relevant memories exist yet.

        Args:
            user_query       : The raw user message.
            user_id          : User identifier.
            include_memories : Set False to skip retrieval (e.g., in tests).
            top_k            : Max memories to inject.
        """
        if not include_memories or not self.is_available:
            return user_query

        memories = self.retrieve_memories_sync(user_id, user_query, top_k=top_k)
        if not memories:
            return user_query

        header = "## Your Previous Context\n" + "".join(
            f"- {m['fact']}\n" for m in memories
        )
        return f"{header}\n## Current Question\n{user_query}"

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/memory/decoupled.py | size=10611 | sha256=203a5a382bd72a312bb26ab0608f018d8cb3d18f4ede7963e2804dff0a00442f =====
"""
common/memory/decoupled.py — Standalone Memory Bank strategy (MemoryMode.DECOUPLED).

Long-term memory backed by a pre-created Vertex AI Memory Bank resource that
lives *outside* Agent Runtime.  The resource is created once via
``deploy/create_memory_bank.py`` and its name is supplied via the
``MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME`` environment variable.

MEMORY CONSOLIDATION STRATEGY — platform-managed, not custom code:
  idle_duration trigger: the Memory Bank server monitors each stream and
  automatically fires memory generation when no new events arrive for
  ``idle_trigger_seconds`` (default 300 s = 5 min).  This covers:
    - Session end        : stream goes idle → auto-consolidate after idle window
    - Orphaned sessions  : same idle-timer mechanism, no client state needed
    - Abandoned streams  : 24-hour platform failsafe (built into Memory Bank)

WORKFLOW (per request):
  1. Pre-query  : retrieve relevant memories → inject as prompt context.
  2. Per-turn   : ingest user message into Memory Bank stream (background task).
  3. Auto-manage: Memory Bank consolidates stream → long-term memories when idle.

Required env vars:
  GCP_PROJECT_ID             — GCP project ID.
  GCP_LOCATION               — GCP region (default: us-central1).
  MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME  — Full resource name of an existing Memory Bank,
                                e.g. projects/…/locations/…/reasoningEngines/…
                                Create once with deploy/create_memory_bank.py.

Optional env vars:
  MEMORY_BANK_IDLE_SECONDS   — Idle-duration trigger in seconds (default: 300).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import uuid
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from common.memory.base import BaseMemoryService, MemoryMode

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Back-reference phrases that signal the user wants long-term context even
# when a short-term session already exists.
_BACK_REF_PHRASES = (
    "as i said",
    "as mentioned",
    "earlier",
    "previous",
    "before",
    "last time",
    "you know my",
    "my preference",
    "my constraints",
    "based on what i told",
    "what did i say",
    "remind me",
)


class DecoupledMemoryService(BaseMemoryService):
    """
    Long-term memory via a standalone (decoupled) Memory Bank resource.

    The Memory Bank resource is managed independently of Agent Runtime —
    it persists across agent redeployments and can be shared across multiple
    agents or agent versions.

    When to use
    -----------
    - ``memory_bank.mode = "decoupled"`` in agent_runtime_config.json.
    - You need memory to survive agent redeployments.
    - Multiple agents or agent versions should share the same memory store.
    - You want custom retrieval control (top_k, scope, etc.).

    Initialisation
    --------------
    ``adk_memory_service`` is always ``None`` — ingestion and retrieval are
    handled manually by this class via ``ingest_turn_sync`` /
    ``retrieve_memories_sync``.

    Args:
        project              : GCP project ID (falls back to ``GCP_PROJECT_ID``).
        location             : GCP region (falls back to ``GCP_LOCATION``).
        idle_trigger_seconds : Idle-duration trigger for memory consolidation.
                               Falls back to ``MEMORY_BANK_IDLE_SECONDS`` env var,
                               then to 300 s.
    """

    adk_memory_service = None  # manual ingestion; not wired into ADK App

    def __init__(
        self,
        project: Optional[str] = None,
        location: Optional[str] = None,
        idle_trigger_seconds: int = 300,
    ) -> None:
        self.project = project or os.environ.get("GCP_PROJECT_ID")
        self.location = location or os.environ.get("GCP_LOCATION", "us-central1")
        self.idle_trigger_seconds = idle_trigger_seconds or int(
            os.environ.get("MEMORY_BANK_IDLE_SECONDS", "300")
        )
        self._client = None
        self._memory_bank_name: Optional[str] = None
        self._initialized = False

        if self.project and self.location:
            self._init_backend()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initialisation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _init_backend(self) -> None:
        """Connect to an existing Memory Bank resource."""
        try:
            import vertexai

            vertexai.init(project=self.project, location=self.location)
            self._client = vertexai.Client(project=self.project, location=self.location)

            resource_name = os.environ.get("MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME", "").strip()
            if not resource_name:
                logger.error(
                    "[DecoupledMemory] MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME is not set. "
                    "Create a Memory Bank with deploy/create_memory_bank.py and "
                    "set this env var. Long-term memory is disabled."
                )
                return

            try:
                mb = self._client.agent_engines.get(name=resource_name)
                self._memory_bank_name = mb.api_resource.name
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error(
                    "[DecoupledMemory] Cannot access resource '%s': %s. "
                    "Verify it exists and the agent identity has "
                    "aiplatform.reasoningEngines.get permission. "
                    "Long-term memory is disabled.",
                    resource_name, exc,
                )
                return

            self._initialized = True
            logger.info(
                "[DecoupledMemory] Ready | resource=%s | idle_trigger=%ds",
                self._memory_bank_name, self.idle_trigger_seconds,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("[DecoupledMemory] Unexpected init failure: %s", exc)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # BaseMemoryService interface
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def mode(self) -> str:
        return MemoryMode.DECOUPLED

    @property
    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return self._initialized and bool(self._memory_bank_name)

    def ingest_turn_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: str,
        text: str,
        event_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Ingest a user turn into the Memory Bank stream (blocking).

        Always call via ``asyncio.to_thread()`` — never on the hot path.

        Memory Bank's server fires consolidation automatically after
        ``idle_trigger_seconds`` of stream inactivity.
        """
        if not self.is_available or not user_id or not text:
            return

        stream_id = session_id or f"user-{user_id}"
        eid = event_id or f"{stream_id}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"

        try:
            self._client.agent_engines.memories.ingest_events(
                name=self._memory_bank_name,
                stream_id=stream_id,
                direct_contents_source={
                    "events": [
                        {
                            "content": {"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": text}]},
                            "event_id": eid,
                        }
                    ]
                },
                generation_trigger_config={
                    "generation_rule": {
                        "idle_duration": f"{self.idle_trigger_seconds}s",
                    }
                },
                scope={"user_id": user_id},
            )
            logger.debug(
                "[DecoupledMemory] Ingested | user=%s session=%s event_id=%s",
                user_id, session_id, eid,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("[DecoupledMemory] ingest_events failed: %s", exc)

    def should_consult_memory(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: Optional[str],
        query_text: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Session-first retrieval policy.

        - No active session → always consult long-term memory.
        - Active session + back-reference phrase → consult long-term memory.
        - Active session + fresh query → rely on session context only.
        """
        if not self.is_available or not user_id:
            return False

        query = (query_text or "").strip().lower()
        if not query:
            return False

        if not session_id:
            return True

        return any(phrase in query for phrase in _BACK_REF_PHRASES)

    def retrieve_memories_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
        top_k: int = 5,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Similarity-search for memories relevant to *query* (blocking).

        Returns ``[]`` silently when no memories exist yet — this is normal
        during early interactions before the first consolidation window.
        """
        if not self.is_available:
            return []

        try:
            logger.info(
                "[DecoupledMemory] Retrieving | user=%s query='%.100s'",
                user_id, query,
            )
            results = self._client.agent_engines.memories.retrieve(
                name=self._memory_bank_name,
                scope={"user_id": user_id},
                similarity_search_params={"search_query": query, "top_k": top_k},
            )
            memories: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
                {
                    "fact": mem.memory.fact,
                    "distance": mem.distance,
                    "created": (
                        str(mem.memory.create_time)
                        if hasattr(mem.memory, "create_time") else None
                    ),
                }
                for mem in list(results)[:top_k]
            ]
            logger.info(
                "[DecoupledMemory] Retrieved %d memories for user=%s",
                len(memories), user_id,
            )
            return memories
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug(
                "[DecoupledMemory] Retrieval not yet available "
                "(expected before first consolidation): %s", exc,
            )
            return []

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/memory/factory.py | size=3975 | sha256=ae5988ffb19c7cd4248015a50e815e0435e2934c665f65c323ba6dcb29388c47 =====
"""
common/memory/factory.py — Factory for creating memory services.

Single entry point for all agent code.  Reads mode from (highest priority first):
  1. Explicit ``mode`` argument passed to ``create_memory_service()``.
  2. ``MEMORY_BANK_MODE`` environment variable.
  3. ``memory_bank.mode`` in agent_runtime_config.json (via ``common.config``).
  4. Hard default: ``"disabled"``.

Usage
-----
Minimal (reads everything from config / env):

    from common.memory import create_memory_service

    memory = create_memory_service()
    app = App(..., memory_service=memory.adk_memory_service)

With explicit overrides (useful in tests or scripts):

    from common.memory import create_memory_service, MemoryMode

    memory = create_memory_service(mode=MemoryMode.DECOUPLED, project="my-proj")
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from typing import Optional

from common.memory.base import BaseMemoryService, MemoryMode

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def create_memory_service(
    mode: Optional[str] = None,
    project: Optional[str] = None,
    location: Optional[str] = None,
    idle_trigger_seconds: Optional[int] = None,
) -> BaseMemoryService:
    """
    Create and return the appropriate memory service for the resolved *mode*.

    Mode resolution order (first non-empty value wins):

    1. ``mode`` argument.
    2. ``MEMORY_BANK_MODE`` env var.
    3. ``memory_bank.mode`` in ``agent_runtime_config.json``.
    4. Default: ``"disabled"``.

    Args:
        mode                 : Explicit mode override (``MemoryMode.*`` constant).
        project              : GCP project ID. Falls back to ``GCP_PROJECT_ID``.
        location             : GCP region.    Falls back to ``GCP_LOCATION``.
        idle_trigger_seconds : Idle-duration consolidation trigger for decoupled
                               mode.  Falls back to config / env / 300 s.

    Returns:
        A :class:`~common.memory.base.BaseMemoryService` instance.
        Always returns a valid object — never raises.

    Examples:
        >>> svc = create_memory_service()
        >>> app = App(..., memory_service=svc.adk_memory_service)
    """
    # ---- resolve mode -------------------------------------------------------
    resolved_mode = (mode or "").strip().lower()

    if not resolved_mode:
        resolved_mode = os.environ.get("MEMORY_BANK_MODE", "").strip().lower()

    cfg_idle: int = 300
    if not resolved_mode:
        try:
            from common.config import cfg
            resolved_mode = cfg.memory_bank.mode.strip().lower()
            cfg_idle = cfg.memory_bank.idle_trigger_seconds
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("[MemoryFactory] Could not read common.config: %s", exc)

    resolved_mode = resolved_mode or MemoryMode.DISABLED

    # ---- resolve idle trigger (decoupled only) -------------------------------
    effective_idle = idle_trigger_seconds or cfg_idle

    logger.info("[MemoryFactory] Initialising memory service | mode=%s", resolved_mode)

    # ---- dispatch -----------------------------------------------------------
    if resolved_mode == MemoryMode.DECOUPLED:
        from common.memory.decoupled import DecoupledMemoryService
        return DecoupledMemoryService(
            project=project,
            location=location,
            idle_trigger_seconds=effective_idle,
        )

    if resolved_mode == MemoryMode.AGENT_RUNTIME:
        from common.memory.agent_runtime import AgentRuntimeMemoryService
        return AgentRuntimeMemoryService(project=project, location=location)

    if resolved_mode != MemoryMode.DISABLED:
        logger.warning(
            "[MemoryFactory] Unknown mode '%s' — falling back to 'disabled'.",
            resolved_mode,
        )

    from common.memory.null import NullMemoryService
    return NullMemoryService()

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/memory/null.py | size=1871 | sha256=a1621b63b2ed27aa9e2b87a9a288ecf45cf363ae9a4bfd2a69e02c8d09376ba7 =====
"""
common/memory/null.py — No-op memory service (MemoryMode.DISABLED).

Used when long-term memory is intentionally turned off.  All methods are
zero-cost no-ops — no imports, no network calls, no cloud credentials needed.
Safe to use in unit tests and local development without any GCP setup.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from common.memory.base import BaseMemoryService, MemoryMode


class NullMemoryService(BaseMemoryService):
    """
    No-op implementation — long-term memory is disabled.

    All interface methods return safe empty values instantly.
    ``adk_memory_service`` is always ``None``.

    When to use
    -----------
    - ``memory_bank.mode = "disabled"`` in agent_runtime_config.json.
    - Unit / integration tests that should not touch the cloud.
    - Local dev when no Memory Bank resource is available.
    """

    adk_memory_service = None

    @property
    def mode(self) -> str:
        return MemoryMode.DISABLED

    @property
    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return False

    def ingest_turn_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: str,
        text: str,
        event_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> None:
        pass  # intentional no-op

    def should_consult_memory(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_id: Optional[str],
        query_text: str,
    ) -> bool:
        return False

    def retrieve_memories_sync(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
        top_k: int = 5,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return []

    def build_context_prompt(
        self,
        user_query: str,
        user_id: str,
        include_memories: bool = True,
        top_k: int = 3,
    ) -> str:
        return user_query

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/plugins.py | size=5104 | sha256=575ad61e9f366a23f90a342b7e37742e41b57ef7f227ae87bd86287720bc5a32 =====
"""
plugins.py — Shared ADK plugins for all agents.

Includes:
  CountInvocationPlugin  — tracks agent/model/tool call counts per container lifetime
  AgentLoggingPlugin     — structured lifecycle logging for any named agent
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Optional

from google.adk.agents.callback_context import CallbackContext
from google.adk.plugins.base_plugin import BasePlugin
from google.genai import types

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CountInvocationPlugin(BasePlugin):
    """Tracks cumulative agent/model/tool invocation counts in-memory.

    Counts reset on container restart. For durability, persist
    metrics:* state keys to Cloud Firestore or BigQuery.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(name="count_invocation_plugin")
        self._agent_count: int = 0
        self._model_count: int = 0
        self._tool_count: int = 0

    async def before_agent_callback(
        self, *, agent, callback_context: CallbackContext, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[types.Content]:
        self._agent_count += 1
        logger.info(
            "[CountPlugin] Agent invocation #%d | agent='%s' | invocation_id=%s",
            self._agent_count,
            agent.name,
            callback_context.invocation_id,
        )
        callback_context.state["metrics:agent_invocations"] = self._agent_count
        callback_context.state["metrics:model_invocations"] = self._model_count
        callback_context.state["metrics:tool_invocations"] = self._tool_count
        return None

    async def before_model_callback(
        self, *, callback_context: CallbackContext, llm_request, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[object]:
        self._model_count += 1
        logger.info("[CountPlugin] Model call #%d", self._model_count)
        return None

    async def before_tool_callback(
        self, *, tool, tool_args: dict, tool_context=None, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[dict]:
        self._tool_count += 1
        logger.info(
            "[CountPlugin] Tool call #%d | tool='%s' | args=%s",
            self._tool_count,
            tool.name,
            tool_args,
        )
        return None


class AgentLoggingPlugin(BasePlugin):
    """Structured lifecycle logging plugin for a named agent."""

    def __init__(self, agent_name: str):
        super().__init__(name=f"{agent_name}_logging_plugin")
        self._agent_name = agent_name

    async def before_agent_callback(
        self, *, agent, callback_context: CallbackContext, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[types.Content]:
        logger.info(
            "[%s] Agent '%s' starting | invocation_id=%s",
            self._agent_name,
            agent.name,
            callback_context.invocation_id,
        )
        return None

    async def after_agent_callback(
        self, *, agent, callback_context: CallbackContext, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[types.Content]:
        logger.info(
            "[%s] Agent '%s' completed | invocation_id=%s",
            self._agent_name,
            agent.name,
            callback_context.invocation_id,
        )
        return None

    async def before_tool_callback(
        self, *, tool, tool_args: dict, tool_context=None, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[dict]:
        logger.info("[%s] Tool '%s' called | args=%s", self._agent_name, tool.name, tool_args)
        return None

    async def after_tool_callback(
        self, *, tool, tool_args: dict, tool_context=None, result: dict = None, **kwargs
    ) -> Optional[dict]:
        result_keys = list(result.keys()) if isinstance(result, dict) else result
        logger.info("[%s] Tool '%s' done | result_keys=%s", self._agent_name, tool.name, result_keys)
        return None

    async def on_event_callback(self, *, event, **kwargs):
        actions = getattr(event, "actions", None) or {}
        state_delta = (
            actions.get("state_delta") if isinstance(actions, dict)
            else getattr(actions, "state_delta", None)
        )
        if state_delta:
            logger.debug("[%s] State delta: %s", self._agent_name, state_delta)

        turn_complete = (
            actions.get("turn_complete") if isinstance(actions, dict)
            else getattr(actions, "turn_complete", False)
        )
        if turn_complete:
            logger.info(
                "[%s] Final response emitted | author=%s",
                self._agent_name,
                getattr(event, "author", "unknown"),
            )
        return None

    async def after_run_callback(
        self,
        callback_context: CallbackContext | None = None,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> None:
        callback_context = callback_context or kwargs.get("callback_context")
        if not callback_context:
            return
        metrics = {k: v for k, v in callback_context.state.items() if k.startswith("metrics:")}
        if metrics:
            logger.info("[%s] Session metrics: %s", self._agent_name, metrics)

===== FILE: eqesmainagent/common/telemetry.py | size=2024 | sha256=0cdd70b2614c7255f3c6e1dca3b10871f37a816c125b15c9ac016f8cad7c5484 =====
import logging
import os

def setup_telemetry(service_name: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    # Always enable Agent Engine's built-in telemetry collector.
    os.environ.setdefault("GOOGLE_CLOUD_AGENT_ENGINE_ENABLE_TELEMETRY", "true")

    if service_name:
        os.environ.setdefault("OTEL_SERVICE_NAME", service_name)

    bucket = os.environ.get("LOGS_BUCKET_NAME")
    capture_content = os.environ.get(
        "OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_CAPTURE_MESSAGE_CONTENT", "false"
    )

    if bucket and capture_content not in ("false", "False", "FALSE", "0"):
        # GCS upload enabled — write GenAI spans as JSONL to the bucket.
        # Hard-cap to NO_CONTENT so prompts/responses are never stored at rest.
        logging.info(
            "GenAI telemetry GCS upload enabled — mode: NO_CONTENT "
            "(span metadata only, prompts/responses excluded)"
        )
        os.environ["OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_CAPTURE_MESSAGE_CONTENT"] = "NO_CONTENT"
        os.environ.setdefault("OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_UPLOAD_FORMAT", "jsonl")
        os.environ.setdefault("OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_COMPLETION_HOOK", "upload")
        os.environ.setdefault(
            "OTEL_SEMCONV_STABILITY_OPT_IN", "gen_ai_latest_experimental"
        )
        commit_sha = os.environ.get("COMMIT_SHA", "dev")
        svc = os.environ.get("OTEL_SERVICE_NAME", "agent")
        os.environ.setdefault(
            "OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES",
            f"service.namespace={svc},service.version={commit_sha}",
        )
        path = os.environ.get("GENAI_TELEMETRY_PATH", "completions")
        os.environ.setdefault(
            "OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_UPLOAD_BASE_PATH",
            f"gs://{bucket}/{path}",
        )
        return bucket
    else:
        logging.info(
            "GenAI telemetry GCS upload disabled. "
            "Set LOGS_BUCKET_NAME and OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_CAPTURE_MESSAGE_CONTENT=NO_CONTENT to enable."
        )
        return None

"""Debug script to test the eqes orchestrator agent and see detailed errors."""
import logging
import sys

# Set up detailed logging
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.DEBUG,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s",
    handlers=[
        logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout),
        logging.FileHandler("debug_agent.log"),
    ],
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Import the agent
from app.agent import root_agent

def test_simple_query():
    """Test a simple query to see if the agent works."""
    logger.info("Starting simple query test...")

    try:
        # Create a test session
        session = root_agent.create_session(user_id="test_user_123")
        logger.info(f"Created session: {session.id if hasattr(session, 'id') else session}")

        # Test query
        test_message = "Hello, what can you help me with?"
        logger.info(f"Sending test message: {test_message}")

        response_parts = []
        for event in root_agent.stream_query(
            user_id="test_user_123",
            session_id=session.id if hasattr(session, "id") else session["id"],
            message=test_message,
        ):
            logger.info(f"Received event type: {type(event)}")
            logger.debug(f"Event content: {event}")

            # Try to extract text
            if isinstance(event, dict):
                content = event.get("content", {})
                parts = content.get("parts", [])
                logger.debug(f"Parts type: {type(parts)}, Parts value: {parts}")

                if parts is None:
                    logger.error("ERROR: parts is None!")
                    continue

                for part in parts:
                    if isinstance(part, dict) and "text" in part:
                        response_parts.append(part["text"])
            else:
                # Object-style event
                content = getattr(event, "content", None)
                if content:
                    parts = getattr(content, "parts", None)
                    logger.debug(f"Parts type: {type(parts)}, Parts value: {parts}")

                    if parts is None:
                        logger.error("ERROR: parts is None!")
                        continue

                    for part in parts:
                        text = getattr(part, "text", None)
                        if text:
                            response_parts.append(text)

        response = "\n".join(response_parts)
        logger.info(f"Final response ({len(response)} chars): {response}")
        print("\n=== RESPONSE ===")
        print(response)

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error during test: %s", exc)
        raise


def test_with_registration():
    """Test with user registration."""
    logger.info("Starting test with user registration...")

    try:
        session = root_agent.create_session(user_id="test_user_456")
        session_id = session.id if hasattr(session, "id") else session["id"]

        # First, register a user
        register_msg = "I'm John Doe, VZ12345, john.doe@verizon.com"
        logger.info(f"Registering user: {register_msg}")

        for event in root_agent.stream_query(
            user_id="test_user_456",
            session_id=session_id,
            message=register_msg,
        ):
            logger.debug(f"Registration event: {event}")

        # Now try a GMeet query
        query_msg = "What GMeet agents are available?"
        logger.info(f"Querying: {query_msg}")

        response_parts = []
        for event in root_agent.stream_query(
            user_id="test_user_456",
            session_id=session_id,
            message=query_msg,
        ):
            logger.debug(f"Query event: {event}")

            if isinstance(event, dict):
                content = event.get("content", {})
                parts = content.get("parts", [])
                if parts is not None:
                    for part in parts:
                        if isinstance(part, dict) and "text" in part:
                            response_parts.append(part["text"])

        response = "\n".join(response_parts)
        logger.info(f"Query response: {response}")
        print("\n=== QUERY RESPONSE ===")
        print(response)

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Error during registration test: %s", exc)
        raise


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("Starting EQES Agent Debugging...")
    print("Logs will be written to debug_agent.log")
    print("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")

    print("Test 1: Simple query")
    try:
        test_simple_query()
    except Exception:
        print("Test 1 FAILED - see debug_agent.log for details")

    print("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")

"""
deploy_eqes_agent.py — Deploy the EQES Main Agent to Google Agent Runtime.

All configuration is read from:
  - config/agent_runtime_config.json  (static infra settings)
  - .env / environment variables       (secrets and environment-specific values)

Run from the project root:
    python -m deploy.deploy_eqes_agent
"""
import datetime
import json
import logging
import os
import sys

from vertexai import types

from common.config import cfg, agent_cfg, vertexai_client
from common.deploy_utils import (
    build_proxy_env_vars,
    validate_labels,
)

_DEPLOY_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
_PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(_DEPLOY_DIR)

_AC = agent_cfg("eqes_orchestrator")

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(name)s - %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _as_rest_env_list(env_vars: dict[str, str]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    return [{"name": key, "value": value} for key, value in env_vars.items()]


def _rest_v1_equivalent_payload(config: dict) -> dict:
    return {
        "displayName": config["display_name"],
        "description": config.get("description", ""),
        "labels": config.get("labels", {}),
        "encryptionSpec": {
            "kmsKeyName": config["encryption_spec"]["kms_key_name"],
        },
        "spec": {
            "agentFramework": config["agent_framework"],
            "identityType": str(config["identity_type"]),
            "containerSpec": {
                "imageUri": config["container_spec"]["image_uri"],
            },
            "deploymentSpec": {
                "minInstances": config["min_instances"],
                "maxInstances": config["max_instances"],
                "resourceLimits": config["resource_limits"],
                "containerConcurrency": config["container_concurrency"],
                "pscInterfaceConfig": config["psc_interface_config"],
                "env": _as_rest_env_list(config.get("env_vars", {})),
            },
        },
    }


def _build_deploy_config() -> dict:
    if not cfg.container_image:
        sys.exit("Missing required config value: container_image")

    container_spec = {
        "image_uri": cfg.container_image,
    }

    deployment_settings = {
        "min_instances": _AC.min_instances,
        "max_instances": _AC.max_instances,
        "resource_limits": {"cpu": _AC.cpu, "memory": _AC.memory},
        "container_concurrency": _AC.container_concurrency,
        "psc_interface_config": {
            "network_attachment": cfg.psc_network_attachment,
            "dns_peering_configs": cfg.dns_peering_configs,
        },
    }

    runtime_env_vars = {
        # GCP identity
        "GCP_PROJECT_ID": cfg.project_id,
        "GCP_LOCATION": cfg.location,
        # Logging level — driven by log_level in agent_runtime_config.json
        "LOG_LEVEL": cfg.log_level.upper(),
        # Agent Runtime tuning
        "NUM_WORKERS": str(_AC.num_workers),
        # Observability
        "OTEL_SERVICE_NAME": _AC.otel_service_name,
        "OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES": f"agent=eqes_orchestrator,project={cfg.project_id}",
        "OTEL_INSTRUMENTATION_GENAI_CAPTURE_MESSAGE_CONTENT": "false",
        "GOOGLE_CLOUD_AGENT_ENGINE_ENABLE_TELEMETRY": "true",
        # LiteLLM / Vegas model
        "LITELLM_MODEL": os.environ["LITELLM_MODEL"],
        "LITELLM_PROXY_API_KEY": os.environ["LITELLM_PROXY_API_KEY"],
        "LITELLM_API_BASE": os.environ["LITELLM_API_BASE"],
        # Vegas direct tool
        "VEGAS_API_KEY": os.environ["VEGAS_API_KEY"],
        "VEGAS_HOST": os.environ.get("VEGAS_HOST", ""),
        "VEGAS_PROJECT": os.environ.get("VEGAS_PROJECT", ""),
        "VEGAS_LOCATION": os.environ.get("VEGAS_LOCATION", ""),
        "VEGAS_MODEL": os.environ.get("VEGAS_MODEL", ""),
        "VEGAS_MODEL_FALLBACKS": os.environ.get("VEGAS_MODEL_FALLBACKS", ""),
        "VEGAS_TIMEOUT": os.environ.get("VEGAS_TIMEOUT", "120.0"),
        "VEGAS_CONNECT_TIMEOUT": os.environ.get("VEGAS_CONNECT_TIMEOUT", "10.0"),
        "VEGAS_MAX_RETRIES": os.environ.get("VEGAS_MAX_RETRIES", "3"),
        # pyvegas — disable DB/A2A features not available in Agent Runtime
        "POSTGRES_LG_CHECKPOINTER_ENABLED": "false",
        "PYVEGAS_LOCAL_MODE": "true",
        "POSTGRES_LG_STORE_ENABLED": "false",
        "POSTGRES_A2A_STORE_ENABLED": "false",
        "ENVIRONMENT": os.environ.get("ENVIRONMENT", "test"),
        # Memory Bank — mode drives which strategy is used at runtime.
        # 'agent_runtime' uses built-in ADK memory (no pre-created resource needed).
        # 'decoupled'     requires MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME (pre-created resource).
        # 'disabled'      no long-term memory.
        "MEMORY_BANK_MODE": cfg.memory_bank.mode,
        "MEMORY_BANK_IDLE_SECONDS": str(cfg.memory_bank.idle_trigger_seconds),
        "MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME": os.environ.get("MEMORY_BANK_RESOURCE_NAME", ""),
        # NOTE: GOOGLE_CLOUD_AGENT_ENGINE_ID is injected automatically by Agent Runtime.
        # We do NOT set it here. See:
        # https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-agent-platform/scale/memory-bank/setup
        # pip
        "PIP_NO_INPUT": "1",
        # Proxy
        **build_proxy_env_vars(),
    }

    # Declare all operations exposed by the BYOC container's main.py endpoints.
    # Required for BYOC/BYOD so Agent Runtime knows what methods the container
    # supports (it cannot introspect the container the way it can managed deployments).
    class_methods = [
        {"api_mode": "",             "name": "get_session"},
        {"api_mode": "",             "name": "list_sessions"},
        {"api_mode": "",             "name": "create_session"},
        {"api_mode": "",             "name": "delete_session"},
        {"api_mode": "async",        "name": "async_get_session"},
        {"api_mode": "async",        "name": "async_list_sessions"},
        {"api_mode": "async",        "name": "async_create_session"},
        {"api_mode": "async",        "name": "async_delete_session"},
        {"api_mode": "async",        "name": "async_add_session_to_memory"},
        {"api_mode": "async",        "name": "async_search_memory"},
        {"api_mode": "stream",       "name": "stream_query"},
        {"api_mode": "async_stream", "name": "async_stream_query"},
        {"api_mode": "async_stream", "name": "streaming_agent_run_with_events"},
    ]

    return {
        "display_name": _AC.display_name,
        "description": _AC.description,
        "container_spec": container_spec,
        **deployment_settings,
        "agent_framework": cfg.agent_framework,
        "labels": cfg.labels,
        "encryption_spec": {
            "kms_key_name": os.environ["GCP_KMS_KEY_NAME"],
        },
        "env_vars": runtime_env_vars,
        "class_methods": class_methods,
    }


def deploy() -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Create the EQES Main Agent on Agent Runtime. Returns (resource_name, endpoint)."""
    required_env = ["GCP_PROJECT_ID", "GCP_KMS_KEY_NAME", "LITELLM_MODEL",
                    "LITELLM_PROXY_API_KEY", "LITELLM_API_BASE", "VEGAS_API_KEY"]
    missing = [k for k in required_env if not os.environ.get(k)]
    if missing:
        sys.exit(f"Missing required environment variables: {missing}")

    validate_labels(cfg.labels)

    logger.info("%s", "=" * 60)
    logger.info("Deploying EQES Main Agent to Google Agent Runtime")
    logger.info("  Project  : %s", cfg.project_id)
    logger.info("  Location : %s", cfg.location)
    logger.info("%s", "=" * 60)

    os.chdir(_PROJECT_ROOT)
    client = vertexai_client()

    config = _build_deploy_config()
    config["identity_type"] = types.IdentityType.AGENT_IDENTITY
    rest_payload = _rest_v1_equivalent_payload(config)

    logger.info("  Creating agent (custom container image deployment)...")
    logger.info("  Container image: %s", config["container_spec"]["image_uri"])
    logger.info("  Resource limits: CPU=%s, Memory=%s",
                config["resource_limits"]["cpu"],
                config["resource_limits"]["memory"])
    logger.debug("  Full config: %s", json.dumps(config, indent=2, default=str))
    logger.debug("  REST v1-equivalent payload: %s", json.dumps(rest_payload, indent=2, default=str))
    remote_agent = client.agent_engines.create(config=config)

    resource_name = remote_agent.api_resource.name
    resource_id = resource_name.split("/")[-1]
    endpoint = cfg.endpoint_url(resource_id)

    logger.info("  Resource name : %s", resource_name)
    logger.info("  Resource ID   : %s", resource_id)
    logger.info("  API endpoint  : %s", endpoint)

    try:
        ops = remote_agent.operation_schemas()
        logger.info("  Operations    : %s", [o.get("name", str(o)) for o in (ops or [])])
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Failed to read operation schemas: %s", exc)

    # Write self-describing deployment record
    output_file = os.path.join(_DEPLOY_DIR, "eqes_agent_deployment_latest.json")
    with open(output_file, "w") as f:
        json.dump({
            "resource_name": resource_name,
            "resource_id": resource_id,
            "endpoint": endpoint,
            "agent": "eqes_orchestrator",
            "deployed_at": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
        }, f, indent=2)
    logger.info("  Deployment record: %s", output_file)

    console_url = (
        f"https://console.cloud.google.com/vertex-ai/agents/agent-engines/"
        f"locations/{cfg.location}/agent-engines/{resource_id}"
        f"/playground?project={cfg.project_id}"
    )
    logger.info("  Console: %s", console_url)
    logger.info("  Next step — update client/.env: EQES_AGENT_RESOURCE_ID=%s", resource_id)

    return resource_name, endpoint


if __name__ == "__main__":
    deploy()

CMEK_KEY_PATH   = "projects/vz-it-np-d0sv-vsadkms-0/locations/us-east4/keyRings/vz-it-np-kr-corp/cryptoKeys/vz-it-np-kms-voev"

AGENT_RESOURCE_NAME = "projects/1041813482705/locations/us-east4/reasoningEngines/7058204943345254400"

    "CORP_PROXY_HOST":    "proxy.ebiz.verizon.com",

        "google-adk[gcp]==2.3.0",

def deploy():
    from app.agent_runtime_app import agent_runtime

    print("Initializing Vertex AI...")
    print(f"  Project: {PROJECT_ID}")
    print(f"  Region : {REGION}")
    print(f"  CMEK   : {CMEK_KEY_PATH}")
    print(f"  Bucket : {STAGING_BUCKET}")

    vertexai.init(project=PROJECT_ID, location=REGION, staging_bucket=STAGING_BUCKET)

    existing = AGENT_RESOURCE_NAME.strip()
    if existing:
        print(f"\nUpdating existing agent: {existing}")
        print("(This can take 5-10 minutes)")
        remote = agent_engines.get(existing)
        remote = remote.update(
            agent_engine=agent_runtime,
            requirements=get_requirements(),
            extra_packages=["./app"],
            display_name=DISPLAY_NAME,
            description="EQES Main Agent — Orchestrator that routes queries to specialized Verizon agents",
            env_vars=CONTAINER_ENV,
        )
        print("\nAgent updated successfully!")
    else:
        print(f"\nCreating new agent '{DISPLAY_NAME}'...")
        print("(This can take 5-10 minutes)")
        remote = agent_engines.create(
            agent_engine=agent_runtime,
            requirements=get_requirements(),
            extra_packages=["./app"],
            display_name=DISPLAY_NAME,
            description="EQES Main Agent — Orchestrator that routes queries to specialized Verizon agents",
            encryption_spec={"kms_key_name": CMEK_KEY_PATH},
            service_account=SERVICE_ACCOUNT,
            env_vars=CONTAINER_ENV,
        )
        print("\nAgent created successfully!")

    print(f"\n  Resource name: {remote.resource_name}")
    print(f"  Display name : {remote.display_name}")
    print("\n  Copy the resource name above into AGENT_RESOURCE_NAME for future updates.")

    with open("agent_runtime_metadata.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump({
            "resource_name": remote.resource_name,
            "display_name": remote.display_name,
            "project": PROJECT_ID,
            "region": REGION,
            "cmek_key": CMEK_KEY_PATH,
        }, f, indent=2)
    print("Metadata saved to agent_runtime_metadata.json")
    return remote


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.environ["GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT"] = PROJECT_ID
    deploy()

#!/bin/bash
# Wait for the reasoning engine to become ACTIVE, then deploy

echo "Waiting for eqesmainagent to finish updating..."
echo "This may take 5-10 minutes."
echo ""

# Wait 2 minutes before first retry
echo "⏱️  Waiting 2 minutes before first retry..."
sleep 120

# Try deployment up to 5 times with 2-minute intervals
for i in {1..5}; do
    echo ""
    echo "========================================="
    echo "Deployment attempt $i of 5"
    echo "========================================="

    python3 deploy_to_agent_runtime.py

    if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
        echo ""
        echo "✅ Deployment successful!"
        exit 0
    fi

    if [ $i -lt 5 ]; then
        echo ""
        echo "⏱️  Deployment failed. Waiting 2 minutes before retry $((i+1))..."
        sleep 120
    fi
done

echo ""
echo "❌ Deployment failed after 5 attempts."
echo "The agent may still be updating. Check status at:"
echo "https://console.cloud.google.com/vertex-ai/agent-builder/engines?project=vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0"
exit 1

===== FILE: gmeetagent/DEPLOYMENT_FIX.md | size=2987 | sha256=103069770e9ded80dd5775f737f6e4c5eebcbbc399ad3af4d2d973474225c48d =====
# Deployment Error Fix

## Error Encountered
```
google.api_core.exceptions.InvalidArgument: 400 The Reasoning Engine failed to be updated.
```

## Root Cause
**Circular/duplicate data in tool return values** - The summary statistics were including full room objects as `best_room` and `worst_room`, which created redundant nested data structures that the Reasoning Engine couldn't serialize properly.

## Fix Applied

### File: `gmeetagent/app/tools.py`

#### Change 1: `get_room_score` function (lines 1109-1120)
**Before:**
```python
summary_stats = {
    "best_room": room_results[0] if room_results else None,  # ❌ Full object
    "worst_room": room_results[-1] if room_results else None,  # ❌ Full object
    ...
}
```

**After:**
```python
summary_stats = {
    "best_room_name": room_results[0]["room_name"] if room_results else None,  # ✅ Name only
    "best_room_score": room_results[0]["avg_gmeet_score"] if room_results else None,  # ✅ Score only
    "worst_room_name": room_results[-1]["room_name"] if room_results else None,  # ✅ Name only
    "worst_room_score": room_results[-1]["avg_gmeet_score"] if room_results else None,  # ✅ Score only
    ...
}
```

#### Change 2: `get_location_room_stats` function (lines 1279-1290)
**Before:**
```python
city_stats.append({
    "best_room": room_avgs[0] if room_avgs else None,  # ❌ Full object
    "worst_room": room_avgs[-1] if room_avgs else None,  # ❌ Full object
    ...
})
```

**After:**
```python
city_stats.append({
    "best_room_name": room_avgs[0]["room_name"] if room_avgs else None,  # ✅ Name only
    "best_room_score": room_avgs[0]["avg_gmeet_score"] if room_avgs else None,  # ✅ Score only
    "worst_room_name": room_avgs[-1]["room_name"] if room_avgs else None,  # ✅ Name only
    "worst_room_score": room_avgs[-1]["avg_gmeet_score"] if room_avgs else None,  # ✅ Score only
    ...
})
```

## Why This Matters

The original code structure:
```json
{
  "summary": {
    "best_room": {
      "room_name": "IN-Chennai-Altius-TR 6402",
      "avg_gmeet_score": 9.75,
      "avg_audio_score": 9.89,
      ...  // Full room object
    }
  },
  "top_rooms": [
    {
      "room_name": "IN-Chennai-Altius-TR 6402",  // ❌ DUPLICATE
      "avg_gmeet_score": 9.75,
      ...
    }
  ]
}
```

The fixed structure:
```json
{
  "summary": {
    "best_room_name": "IN-Chennai-Altius-TR 6402",  // ✅ Simple reference
    "best_room_score": 9.75
  },
  "top_rooms": [
    {
      "room_name": "IN-Chennai-Altius-TR 6402",
      "avg_gmeet_score": 9.75,
      ...
    }
  ]
}
```

All syntax checks passed. The code is ready for deployment:

# GMeet Agent Response Generation Fixes

## Problem
The agent was successfully executing tool functions (`get_gmeet_score`, `get_room_score`, etc.) but **not generating final text responses** to present the data to users.

### Root Causes Identified:
1. **Response payload too large** - Functions were returning excessive data (e.g., 34 rooms with full details = 20KB+ JSON)
2. **Model context overflow** - Large payloads overwhelmed the model's ability to generate coherent summaries
3. **Missing explicit response requirement** - Agent instructions didn't emphasize the need to ALWAYS respond
4. **No result summarization** - Raw data was returned without aggregated summaries

## Fixes Applied

### 1. **Reduced Payload Sizes**

#### `get_gmeet_score` (tools.py:482)
- Changed from returning 10 meetings → **5 meetings**
- Reduces response size by 50%

#### `get_room_score` (tools.py:1074-1106)
- **Before**: Returned ALL rooms (34+ for Chennai) with full details
- **After**: Returns structured summary with:
  - Summary statistics (total, avg, distribution)
  - Top 10 rooms only
  - Bottom 5 rooms only
  - Reduces payload by ~70%

#### `get_team_gmeet_scores` (tools.py:869-897)
- **Before**: Returned all team members with full details
- **After**: Limits to:
  - Top 15 member details
  - Top 10 performers
  - Bottom 5 performers
  - First 5 members without data

#### `get_location_room_stats` (tools.py:1236-1249)
- Per city: Top 5 rooms + Bottom 3 rooms (was all rooms)
- Reduces payload for multi-city queries

### 2. **Simplified Data Structures**

#### Room Results (tools.py:1075-1089)
**Removed** from each room:
- Full `category_scores` object with nested sub_scores
- Complete `issues_detected` array

**Replaced with**:
- Simple average scores (audio, video, screencast)
- `issues_count` (integer)
- `top_issue` (single most important issue)

**Result**: 60% smaller per-room payload

### 3. **Added Explicit Response Requirements**

#### Agent Instruction (agent.py:26-27)
Added critical directive at the top:
```
**CRITICAL: You MUST ALWAYS generate a natural language response to the user 
after EVERY tool execution. Never end the conversation without responding to the user.**
```

#### Room Results Guidance (agent.py:201-211)
Added specific instructions:
- **ALWAYS start with a summary**
- Provide score distribution
- Highlight top 3-5 performers
- Call out bottom 3-5 performers
- **IMPORTANT**: Must respond even if data is large

### 4. **Enhanced Logging**

#### `get_gmeet_score` (tools.py:372, 495-497)
```python
logger.info(f"get_gmeet_score called with eid={eid}, days={days}...")
logger.info(f"get_gmeet_score returning: total_meetings={total_meetings}, score={overall_score}")
```

#### `get_room_score` (tools.py:1017, 1111-1112)
```python
logger.info(f"get_room_score called with room_name={room_name}, location={location}, city={city}")
logger.info(f"get_room_score returning: {total_rooms} rooms, avg_score={avg_score}")
```

### 5. **Improved Error Handling**

All tool functions now use:
```python
except Exception as exc:
    logger.error("function_name failed: %s", exc, exc_info=True)  # Stack trace included
```

## Files Modified

1. **`gmeetagent/app/tools.py`**
   - Lines 372: Added entry logging to `get_gmeet_score`
   - Lines 482: Reduced meetings from 10 → 5
   - Lines 495-500: Added success logging
   - Lines 869-897: Limited team member results
   - Lines 1017: Added entry logging to `get_room_score`
   - Lines 1074-1106: Restructured room results with summary
   - Lines 1111-1113: Added success logging
   - Lines 1236-1249: Simplified location stats

2. **`gmeetagent/app/agent.py`**
   - Lines 26-27: Added critical response requirement
   - Lines 201-211: Enhanced room results guidance

## Expected Impact

### Before:

- ✅ Tool executes successfully (34 rooms, 185 meetings)
- ❌ **NO response to user** (silent failure)

### After:

  1. IN-Chennai-Altius-TR 6402: 9.75/10 (Excellent) - 4 meetings
  2. IN-Chennai-OTP-CR 1003: 9.39/10 (Excellent) - 10 meetings
  3. IN-Chennai-OTP-CR 4002: 9.32/10 (Excellent) - 6 meetings
  
  Rooms Needing Attention:
  1. IN-Chennai-MR-3001: 5.54/10 (Poor) - Audio issues detected
  2. IN-Chennai-OTP-CR 7001: 5.58/10 (Poor) - High jitter and packet loss
