"""Google Meet Media API client using WebRTC (aiortc).

Joins a Google Meet as a bot participant, receives audio streams,
and can inject TTS audio back into the meeting.
"""
import asyncio
import logging
from collections.abc import Callable, Awaitable

import numpy as np
from aiortc import (
    MediaStreamTrack,
    RTCPeerConnection,
    RTCSessionDescription,
)
from aiortc.mediastreams import AudioStreamTrack
from av import AudioFrame

from app.config import (
    GCP_PROJECT,
    MEET_BOT_DISPLAY_NAME,
    TTS_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ,
    WEBRTC_AUDIO_TRANSCEIVER_COUNT,
    WEBRTC_ICE_RECONNECT_DELAY_SECONDS,
    WEBRTC_ICE_RECONNECT_MAX_RETRIES,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

AudioCallback = Callable[[bytes], Awaitable[None]]


class TtsAudioTrack(MediaStreamTrack):
    """Custom audio track that plays TTS frames then silence."""

    kind = "audio"

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self._queue: asyncio.Queue[list[bytes]] = asyncio.Queue()
        self._current_frames: list[bytes] = []
        self._frame_index = 0
        self._pts = 0
        self._sample_rate = TTS_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ
        self._samples_per_frame = (self._sample_rate * 20) // 1000  # 20ms frames

    async def recv(self) -> AudioFrame:
        if not self._current_frames or self._frame_index >= len(self._current_frames):
            try:
                self._current_frames = self._queue.get_nowait()
                self._frame_index = 0
            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:
                self._current_frames = []
                self._frame_index = 0

        if self._current_frames and self._frame_index < len(self._current_frames):
            pcm_data = self._current_frames[self._frame_index]
            self._frame_index += 1
        else:
            pcm_data = b"\x00\x00" * self._samples_per_frame

        samples = np.frombuffer(pcm_data, dtype=np.int16)
        frame = AudioFrame.from_ndarray(
            samples.reshape(1, -1), format="s16", layout="mono"
        )
        frame.sample_rate = self._sample_rate
        frame.pts = self._pts
        self._pts += self._samples_per_frame

        await asyncio.sleep(20 / 1000)
        return frame

    def enqueue_audio(self, pcm_frames: list[bytes]) -> None:
        self._queue.put_nowait(pcm_frames)


class MeetMediaBot:
    """Connects to Google Meet via the Meet Media API (WebRTC)."""

    def __init__(self, space_name: str, on_audio_frame: AudioCallback):
        self._space_name = space_name
        self._on_audio_frame = on_audio_frame
        self._pc: RTCPeerConnection | None = None
        self._tts_track: TtsAudioTrack | None = None
        self._connected = False
        self._reconnect_count = 0
        self._receive_tasks: list[asyncio.Task] = []

    @property
    def connected(self) -> bool:
        return self._connected

    async def connect(self, credentials) -> None:
        """Establish WebRTC connection to Meet via the Media API.

        Args:
            credentials: Google auth credentials for Meet API access.
        """
        self._pc = RTCPeerConnection()

        for _ in range(WEBRTC_AUDIO_TRANSCEIVER_COUNT):
            self._pc.addTransceiver("audio", direction="recvonly")

        self._tts_track = TtsAudioTrack()
        self._pc.addTrack(self._tts_track)

        self._pc.addTransceiver("video", direction="recvonly")

        @self._pc.on("track")
        async def on_track(track: MediaStreamTrack):
            if track.kind == "audio":
                task = asyncio.create_task(self._read_audio_track(track))
                self._receive_tasks.append(task)
            logger.info("Received %s track", track.kind)

        @self._pc.on("iceconnectionstatechange")
        async def on_ice_state():
            state = self._pc.iceConnectionState
            logger.info("ICE connection state: %s", state)
            if state in ("disconnected", "failed"):
                await self._handle_disconnect()

        offer = await self._pc.createOffer()
        await self._pc.setLocalDescription(offer)

        logger.info("Sending SDP offer to Meet Media API for space: %s", self._space_name)

        try:
            from google.apps.meet_v2beta import SpacesServiceAsyncClient
            from google.apps.meet_v2beta.types import ConnectActiveConferenceRequest

            client = SpacesServiceAsyncClient(credentials=credentials)
            response = await client.connect_active_conference(
                ConnectActiveConferenceRequest(
                    name=self._space_name,
                    offer=self._pc.localDescription.sdp,
                )
            )

            answer = RTCSessionDescription(sdp=response.answer, type="answer")
            await self._pc.setRemoteDescription(answer)
            self._connected = True
            self._reconnect_count = 0
            logger.info("WebRTC connection established to Meet")

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Meet Media API connection failed: %s", exc)
            await self.disconnect()
            raise

    async def _read_audio_track(self, track: MediaStreamTrack) -> None:
        try:
            while True:
                frame = await track.recv()
                pcm = frame.to_ndarray().flatten()
                pcm_bytes = pcm.astype(np.int16).tobytes()
                await self._on_audio_frame(pcm_bytes)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Audio track ended: %s", exc)

    async def _handle_disconnect(self) -> None:
        if self._reconnect_count >= WEBRTC_ICE_RECONNECT_MAX_RETRIES:
            logger.error("Max reconnection attempts reached, giving up")
            self._connected = False
            return

        self._reconnect_count += 1
        logger.warning(
            "ICE disconnected, attempt %d/%d in %ds",
            self._reconnect_count,
            WEBRTC_ICE_RECONNECT_MAX_RETRIES,
            WEBRTC_ICE_RECONNECT_DELAY_SECONDS,
        )
        await asyncio.sleep(WEBRTC_ICE_RECONNECT_DELAY_SECONDS)

    async def inject_audio(self, pcm_frames: list[bytes]) -> None:
        """Inject TTS audio frames into the meeting.

        Args:
            pcm_frames: List of raw PCM byte frames (20ms each, 48kHz, mono, 16-bit).
        """
        if self._tts_track and self._connected:
            self._tts_track.enqueue_audio(pcm_frames)
            logger.info("Injected %d audio frames into meeting", len(pcm_frames))

    async def disconnect(self) -> None:
        self._connected = False
        for task in self._receive_tasks:
            task.cancel()
        self._receive_tasks.clear()
        if self._pc:
            await self._pc.close()
            self._pc = None
        logger.info("Disconnected from Meet")


def parse_meet_link(meet_link: str) -> str:
    """Extract the meeting code from a Google Meet link.

    Args:
        meet_link: Full Meet URL (e.g., https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij).

    Returns:
        The meeting code (e.g., abc-defg-hij).

    Raises:
        ValueError: If the link format is invalid.
    """
    import re

    patterns = [
        r"meet\.google\.com/([a-z]{3}-[a-z]{4}-[a-z]{3})",
        r"meet\.google\.com/([a-zA-Z0-9\-]+)",
    ]
    for pattern in patterns:
        match = re.search(pattern, meet_link)
        if match:
            return match.group(1)
    raise ValueError(f"Invalid Meet link format: {meet_link}")


async def resolve_space_name(meeting_code: str, credentials) -> str:
    """Resolve a meeting code to a Meet API space resource name.

    Args:
        meeting_code: The meeting code (e.g., abc-defg-hij).
        credentials: Google auth credentials.

    Returns:
        Space resource name (e.g., spaces/abc123).
    """
    try:
        from google.apps.meet_v2 import SpacesServiceAsyncClient
        from google.apps.meet_v2.types import GetSpaceRequest

        client = SpacesServiceAsyncClient(credentials=credentials)
        space = await client.get_space(
            GetSpaceRequest(name=f"spaces/{meeting_code}")
        )
        return space.name
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Could not resolve space name via API: %s", exc)
        return f"spaces/{meeting_code}"

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/meet_bot_fallback.py | size=8479 | sha256=7e44b2afa0a64d31cc3b9da915156c8aee0c06bffd4d06f7202a19809698162e =====
"""Playwright browser automation fallback for Google Meet.

Used when the Meet Media API is unavailable. Joins Meet via headless
Chrome, scrapes captions for transcription, responds via chat.
"""
import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any

from app.config import (
    CAPTION_POLL_INTERVAL_MS,
    MEET_BOT_DISPLAY_NAME,
    PLAYWRIGHT_HEADLESS,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class PlaywrightMeetBot:
    """Joins Google Meet via browser automation and scrapes captions."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._browser = None
        self._page = None
        self._context = None
        self._running = False
        self._seen_captions: set[str] = set()
        self._poll_task: asyncio.Task | None = None

    @property
    def connected(self) -> bool:
        return self._running

    async def join(self, meet_link: str, display_name: str = MEET_BOT_DISPLAY_NAME) -> None:
        """Join a Google Meet via headless browser.

        Args:
            meet_link: Full Google Meet URL.
            display_name: Name displayed in the meeting.
        """
        from playwright.async_api import async_playwright

        pw = await async_playwright().start()
        self._browser = await pw.chromium.launch(
            headless=PLAYWRIGHT_HEADLESS,
            args=[
                "--use-fake-ui-for-media-stream",
                "--use-fake-device-for-media-stream",
                "--disable-gpu",
                "--no-sandbox",
                "--disable-dev-shm-usage",
                "--disable-extensions",
                "--disable-notifications",
            ],
        )

        self._context = await self._browser.new_context(
            permissions=["camera", "microphone"],
            user_agent=(
                "Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) AppleWebKit/537.36 "
                "(KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/125.0.0.0 Safari/537.36"
            ),
        )
        self._page = await self._context.new_page()

        logger.info("Navigating to Meet: %s", meet_link)
        await self._page.goto(meet_link, wait_until="networkidle", timeout=30000)
        await asyncio.sleep(3)

        try:
            name_input = await self._page.query_selector('input[aria-label="Your name"]')
            if name_input:
                await name_input.fill("")
                await name_input.type(display_name, delay=50)
                logger.info("Entered display name: %s", display_name)
        except Exception:
            logger.debug("No name input found (may already be signed in)")

        try:
            await self._dismiss_pre_join_dialogs()
        except Exception:
            logger.debug("No pre-join dialogs to dismiss")

        join_selectors = [
            'button:has-text("Join now")',
            'button:has-text("Ask to join")',
            'button:has-text("Join")',
            '[data-is-muted] + div button',
        ]
        joined = False
        for selector in join_selectors:
            try:
                btn = await self._page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=5000)
                if btn:
                    await btn.click()
                    joined = True
                    logger.info("Clicked join button: %s", selector)
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue

        if not joined:
            raise RuntimeError("Could not find join button in Google Meet")

        await asyncio.sleep(5)

        try:
            await self._enable_captions()
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Could not enable captions: %s", exc)

        self._running = True
        logger.info("Successfully joined Meet via browser")

    async def _dismiss_pre_join_dialogs(self) -> None:
        dismiss_selectors = [
            'button:has-text("Got it")',
            'button:has-text("Dismiss")',
            'button:has-text("Close")',
        ]
        for selector in dismiss_selectors:
            try:
                btn = await self._page.query_selector(selector)
                if btn:
                    await btn.click()
                    await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
            except Exception:
                pass

    async def _enable_captions(self) -> None:
        cc_selectors = [
            'button[aria-label="Turn on captions"]',
            'button[aria-label="Turn on captions (c)"]',
            'button:has-text("Turn on captions")',
        ]
        for selector in cc_selectors:
            try:
                btn = await self._page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=5000)
                if btn:
                    await btn.click()
                    logger.info("Captions enabled")
                    return
            except Exception:
                continue
        logger.warning("Could not find captions button")

    async def poll_captions(self) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        """Poll the DOM for new caption text.

        Returns:
            List of new caption entries with speaker and text.
        """
        if not self._page or not self._running:
            return []

        new_entries = []
        try:
            caption_elements = await self._page.query_selector_all(
                '[class*="caption"], [class*="subtitle"], [data-self-name]'
            )

            for elem in caption_elements:
                text = await elem.inner_text()
                text = text.strip()
                if not text or text in self._seen_captions:
                    continue
                self._seen_captions.add(text)

                parts = text.split("\n", 1)
                if len(parts) == 2:
                    speaker, content = parts[0].strip(), parts[1].strip()
                else:
                    speaker, content = "Unknown", text

                if content:
                    new_entries.append({"speaker": speaker, "text": content})

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Caption polling error: %s", exc)

        return new_entries

    async def send_chat_message(self, message: str) -> bool:
        """Send a message in the Meet chat.

        Args:
            message: Text to send.

        Returns:
            True if sent successfully.
        """
        if not self._page or not self._running:
            return False

        try:
            chat_selectors = [
                'button[aria-label="Chat with everyone"]',
                'button[aria-label="Chat"]',
                'button:has-text("Chat")',
            ]
            for selector in chat_selectors:
                try:
                    btn = await self._page.query_selector(selector)
                    if btn:
                        await btn.click()
                        await asyncio.sleep(1)
                        break
                except Exception:
                    continue

            input_selectors = [
                'textarea[aria-label="Send a message to everyone"]',
                'textarea[aria-label="Send a message"]',
                'textarea',
            ]
            for selector in input_selectors:
                try:
                    text_input = await self._page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=3000)
                    if text_input:
                        await text_input.type(message, delay=20)
                        await self._page.keyboard.press("Enter")
                        logger.info("Chat message sent: %s", message[:80])
                        return True
                except Exception:
                    continue

            logger.warning("Could not find chat input")
            return False

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Failed to send chat message: %s", exc)
            return False

    async def leave(self) -> None:
        """Leave the meeting and close the browser."""
        self._running = False

        if self._poll_task:
            self._poll_task.cancel()

        if self._page:
            try:
                leave_btn = await self._page.query_selector(
                    'button[aria-label="Leave call"]'
                )
                if leave_btn:
                    await leave_btn.click()
                    await asyncio.sleep(1)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if self._context:
            await self._context.close()
        if self._browser:
            await self._browser.close()
        logger.info("Left meeting and closed browser")

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/meeting_manager.py | size=13905 | sha256=56b800866fff22932bb3cde76e5cfda84fafde60c3d165553b62b90e9b88d1b6 =====
"""Central orchestrator: wires bot + STT + TTS + name detection + notifications."""
import asyncio
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

import google.auth

from app import session_store
from app.config import (
    CAPTION_POLL_INTERVAL_MS,
    GCP_PROJECT,
    GCP_REGION,
    GEMINI_MODEL,
    GEMINI_MAX_RESPONSE_TOKENS,
    MEET_BOT_DISPLAY_NAME,
    MEET_MEDIA_API_ENABLED,
)
from app.meet_bot import MeetMediaBot, parse_meet_link, resolve_space_name
from app.meet_bot_fallback import PlaywrightMeetBot
from app.name_detector import NameDetector
from app.notification_tools import notify_name_mention
from app.stt_streamer import SttStreamer
from app.tts_speaker import pcm_to_frames, synthesize_speech

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MeetingManager:
    """Singleton that manages one active meeting at a time."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._media_bot: MeetMediaBot | None = None
        self._fallback_bot: PlaywrightMeetBot | None = None
        self._stt: SttStreamer | None = None
        self._name_detector: NameDetector | None = None
        self._active_meeting_id: str | None = None
        self._caption_task: asyncio.Task | None = None
        self._credentials = None

    def _get_credentials(self):
        if self._credentials is None:
            self._credentials, _ = google.auth.default()
        return self._credentials

    async def join_meeting(
        self,
        meet_link: str,
        user_name: str,
        wake_name: str,
        user_email: str = "",
        slack_channel_id: str = "",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        active = session_store.get_active_session()
        if active:
            return {
                "meeting_id": active.meeting_id,
                "status": "already_active",
                "error": "Already in a meeting. Leave the current meeting first.",
            }

        meeting_id = session_store.create_session(
            meet_link=meet_link,
            user_name=user_name,
            wake_name=wake_name,
            user_email=user_email,
            slack_channel_id=slack_channel_id,
        )
        self._active_meeting_id = meeting_id
        self._name_detector = NameDetector(wake_name)

        session_store.update_status(meeting_id, "joining")

        if MEET_MEDIA_API_ENABLED:
            try:
                result = await self._join_via_media_api(meeting_id, meet_link)
                return result
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning("Meet Media API failed: %s, trying fallback", exc)

        try:
            result = await self._join_via_fallback(meeting_id, meet_link)
            return result
        except Exception as exc:
            session_store.update_status(meeting_id, "failed")
            self._active_meeting_id = None
            return {
                "meeting_id": meeting_id,
                "status": "failed",
                "mode": "none",
                "error": f"Both Media API and browser fallback failed: {exc}",
            }

    async def _join_via_media_api(
        self, meeting_id: str, meet_link: str
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        meeting_code = parse_meet_link(meet_link)
        credentials = self._get_credentials()

        space_name = await resolve_space_name(meeting_code, credentials)
        session_store.update_space_name(meeting_id, space_name)

        async def on_audio(pcm_bytes: bytes) -> None:
            if self._stt:
                await self._stt.feed_audio(pcm_bytes)

        self._media_bot = MeetMediaBot(space_name, on_audio_frame=on_audio)

        def on_transcript(speaker: str, text: str, is_final: bool) -> None:
            ts = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
            session_store.append_transcript(meeting_id, speaker, text, ts)
            if is_final and self._name_detector:
                mention = self._name_detector.check(text)
                if mention:
                    asyncio.create_task(
                        self._handle_name_mention(meeting_id, mention, text)
                    )

        self._stt = SttStreamer(on_transcript=on_transcript)
        await self._stt.start()
        await self._media_bot.connect(credentials)

        session_store.update_status(meeting_id, "active")
        session_store.update_mode(meeting_id, "media_api")

        return {
            "meeting_id": meeting_id,
            "status": "active",
            "mode": "media_api",
            "error": None,
        }

    async def _join_via_fallback(
        self, meeting_id: str, meet_link: str
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._fallback_bot = PlaywrightMeetBot()
        await self._fallback_bot.join(meet_link, MEET_BOT_DISPLAY_NAME)

        session_store.update_status(meeting_id, "active")
        session_store.update_mode(meeting_id, "browser_fallback")

        self._caption_task = asyncio.create_task(
            self._caption_poll_loop(meeting_id)
        )

        return {
            "meeting_id": meeting_id,
            "status": "active",
            "mode": "browser_fallback",
            "note": "Browser mode: captions for transcript, chat for responses (no audio injection)",
            "error": None,
        }

    async def _caption_poll_loop(self, meeting_id: str) -> None:
        while self._fallback_bot and self._fallback_bot.connected:
            try:
                entries = await self._fallback_bot.poll_captions()
                for entry in entries:
                    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
                    session_store.append_transcript(
                        meeting_id, entry["speaker"], entry["text"], ts
                    )
                    if self._name_detector:
                        mention = self._name_detector.check(entry["text"])
                        if mention:
                            asyncio.create_task(
                                self._handle_name_mention(
                                    meeting_id, mention, entry["text"]
                                )
                            )
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug("Caption poll error: %s", exc)
            await asyncio.sleep(CAPTION_POLL_INTERVAL_MS / 1000)

    async def _handle_name_mention(self, meeting_id: str, mention, full_text: str) -> None:
        session = session_store.get_session(meeting_id)
        if not session:
            return

        logger.info(
            "Name '%s' detected in meeting %s: %s",
            mention.name_matched,
            meeting_id,
            mention.context_text[:100],
        )

        response_text = await self._generate_response(session, mention.question)

        session_store.record_mention(
            meeting_id,
            datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
            mention.context_text,
            response_text,
        )

        if session.mode == "media_api" and self._media_bot and self._media_bot.connected:
            try:
                pcm = synthesize_speech(response_text)
                frames = pcm_to_frames(pcm)
                await self._media_bot.inject_audio(frames)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("TTS injection failed: %s", exc)
        elif session.mode == "browser_fallback" and self._fallback_bot:
            try:
                await self._fallback_bot.send_chat_message(
                    f"[{session.user_name}]: {response_text}"
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("Chat message failed: %s", exc)

        try:
            notify_name_mention(
                meeting_id=meeting_id,
                mention_text=mention.context_text,
                question=mention.question,
                response_given=response_text,
                user_email=session.user_email,
                slack_channel_id=session.slack_channel_id,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Notification failed: %s", exc)

    async def _generate_response(self, session, question: str) -> str:
        try:
            from google import genai

            client = genai.Client(
                vertexai=True, project=GCP_PROJECT, location=GCP_REGION
            )

            context_str = "\n".join(
                f"- {k}: {v}" for k, v in session.context.items()
            )
            if not context_str:
                context_str = "(No specific context provided)"

            prompt = (
                f"You are answering on behalf of {session.user_name} in a meeting. "
                f"Be concise (2-3 sentences max), professional, and natural.\n\n"
                f"Context provided by {session.user_name}:\n{context_str}\n\n"
                f"Someone in the meeting said something directed at {session.user_name}: "
                f'"{question}"\n\n'
                f"Provide a response as if you are {session.user_name}. "
                f"If the context doesn't have enough information, say: "
                f'"I\'ll need to check on that and get back to you."'
            )

            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=GEMINI_MODEL,
                contents=prompt,
                config={"max_output_tokens": GEMINI_MAX_RESPONSE_TOKENS},
            )
            return response.text.strip()

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Gemini response generation failed: %s", exc)
            return "I'll need to check on that and get back to you."

    async def leave_meeting(self, meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        session = session_store.get_session(meeting_id)
        if not session:
            return {"status": "not_found", "error": f"Meeting {meeting_id} not found"}

        if self._stt:
            await self._stt.stop()
            self._stt = None

        if self._media_bot:
            await self._media_bot.disconnect()
            self._media_bot = None

        if self._caption_task:
            self._caption_task.cancel()
            self._caption_task = None

        if self._fallback_bot:
            await self._fallback_bot.leave()
            self._fallback_bot = None

        session_store.update_status(meeting_id, "ended")
        self._active_meeting_id = None
        self._name_detector = None

        transcript = session_store.get_transcript(meeting_id)
        return {
            "status": "ended",
            "meeting_id": meeting_id,
            "transcript_length": len(transcript),
            "name_mentions": len(session.name_mentions),
            "error": None,
        }

    def get_transcript(self, meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        session = session_store.get_session(meeting_id)
        if not session:
            return {"transcript": [], "length": 0, "error": f"Meeting {meeting_id} not found"}
        transcript = session_store.get_transcript(meeting_id)
        return {"transcript": transcript, "length": len(transcript), "error": None}

    async def generate_summary(self, meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        session = session_store.get_session(meeting_id)
        if not session:
            return {"summary": "", "error": f"Meeting {meeting_id} not found"}

        transcript = session_store.get_transcript(meeting_id)
        if not transcript:
            return {"summary": "No transcript available", "action_items": [], "error": None}

        transcript_text = "\n".join(
            f"[{t['timestamp']}] {t['speaker']}: {t['text']}" for t in transcript
        )

        try:
            from google import genai

            client = genai.Client(
                vertexai=True, project=GCP_PROJECT, location=GCP_REGION
            )

            prompt = (
                "Summarize this meeting transcript. Provide:\n"
                "1. **Key Discussion Points** (3-5 bullets)\n"
                "2. **Decisions Made** (if any)\n"
                "3. **Action Items** (with owner if identifiable)\n"
                "4. **Follow-up Items** (things to revisit)\n\n"
                f"Meeting transcript:\n{transcript_text[:10000]}"
            )

            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=GEMINI_MODEL, contents=prompt
            )

            action_items = []
            for line in response.text.split("\n"):
                line = line.strip()
                if any(
                    marker in line.lower()
                    for marker in ["action:", "todo:", "follow up:", "- [ ]"]
                ) or (line.startswith("- ") and "should" in line.lower()):
                    action_items.append(line.lstrip("- ").strip())

            return {
                "summary": response.text.strip(),
                "action_items": action_items,
                "transcript_length": len(transcript),
                "name_mentions": len(session.name_mentions),
                "duration": (
                    str(session.ended_at - session.started_at)
                    if session.ended_at and session.started_at
                    else "ongoing"
                ),
                "error": None,
            }

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Summary generation failed: %s", exc)
            return {"summary": "", "action_items": [], "error": str(exc)}

    def update_wake_name(self, meeting_id: str, wake_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        session = session_store.get_session(meeting_id)
        if not session:
            return {"status": "not_found", "error": f"Meeting {meeting_id} not found"}
        session_store.update_wake_name(meeting_id, wake_name)
        if self._name_detector:
            self._name_detector.wake_name = wake_name
        return {"status": "updated", "wake_name": wake_name, "error": None}


_manager = MeetingManager()


def get_manager() -> MeetingManager:
    return _manager

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/name_detector.py | size=3158 | sha256=b153be11be240a73bf89aa54a90e57db674f87ec473bf26df49441ed93efabbf =====
"""Wake-name detection on meeting transcript text."""
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class NameMention:
    name_matched: str
    context_text: str
    question: str
    position: int


class NameDetector:
    def __init__(self, wake_name: str):
        self._wake_name = wake_name.strip().lower()
        parts = self._wake_name.split()
        self._patterns: list[re.Pattern] = []
        if len(parts) >= 2:
            self._patterns.append(
                re.compile(r"\b" + re.escape(" ".join(parts)) + r"\b", re.IGNORECASE)
            )
        for part in parts:
            if len(part) >= 3:
                self._patterns.append(
                    re.compile(r"\b" + re.escape(part) + r"\b", re.IGNORECASE)
                )

    @property
    def wake_name(self) -> str:
        return self._wake_name

    @wake_name.setter
    def wake_name(self, value: str) -> None:
        self.__init__(value)

    def check(self, text: str) -> NameMention | None:
        if not text or not self._patterns:
            return None
        for pattern in self._patterns:
            match = pattern.search(text)
            if match:
                context = self._extract_context(text, match.start())
                question = self._extract_question(text, match.end())
                return NameMention(
                    name_matched=match.group(),
                    context_text=context,
                    question=question,
                    position=match.start(),
                )
        return None

    def _extract_context(self, text: str, mention_pos: int) -> str:
        sentences = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", text)
        context_parts = []
        char_count = 0
        for sentence in sentences:
            start = char_count
            end = start + len(sentence)
            if start <= mention_pos <= end:
                idx = sentences.index(sentence)
                if idx > 0:
                    context_parts.append(sentences[idx - 1])
                context_parts.append(sentence)
                if idx < len(sentences) - 1:
                    context_parts.append(sentences[idx + 1])
                return " ".join(context_parts)
            char_count = end + 1
        return text[:300]

    def _extract_question(self, text: str, after_name_pos: int) -> str:
        remaining = text[after_name_pos:].strip()
        if not remaining:
            sentences = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", text)
            for s in sentences:
                if "?" in s or any(
                    w in s.lower()
                    for w in ["what", "how", "when", "where", "why", "can", "could",
                              "would", "should", "do you", "think", "update", "status"]
                ):
                    return s.strip()
            return text[-200:].strip()

        sentences = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", remaining)
        question_parts = []
        for s in sentences[:3]:
            question_parts.append(s)
            if "?" in s:
                break
        result = " ".join(question_parts).strip(" ,;:")
        return result if result else remaining[:200]

"""Email and Slack notification tools for meeting alerts."""
import logging
import smtplib
from datetime import datetime
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from typing import Any

import requests as http_requests
from slack_sdk import WebClient
from slack_sdk.errors import SlackApiError

from app.config import (
    DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,
    EMAIL_SERVICE_URL,
    PROXIES,
    SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
    SMTP_HOST,
    SMTP_PORT,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def send_slack_notification(
    channel_id: str, message: str, title: str = "Meeting Alert"
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Send a Slack notification about meeting activity.

    Args:
        channel_id: Slack channel ID.
        message: Notification message.
        title: Bold header above the message.

    Returns:
        dict with status, error.
    """
    if not SLACK_BOT_TOKEN:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "SLACK_BOT_TOKEN not configured"}
    try:
        client = WebClient(token=SLACK_BOT_TOKEN)
        blocks = [
            {"type": "header", "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": title, "emoji": True}},
            {"type": "section", "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": message}},
            {"type": "divider"},
            {"type": "context", "elements": [{"type": "mrkdwn", "text": f"_Meeting AI Assistant | {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}_"}]},
        ]
        resp = client.chat_postMessage(channel=channel_id, blocks=blocks, text=message)
        return {"status": "sent", "ts": resp["ts"], "error": None}
    except SlackApiError as e:
        logger.error("Slack notification failed: %s", e.response["error"])
        return {"status": "failed", "error": e.response["error"]}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("Slack error: %s", e)
        return {"status": "failed", "error": str(e)}


def send_email_notification(
    to_email: str, subject: str, body: str, is_html: bool = False
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Send an email notification.

    Args:
        to_email: Recipient email address.
        subject: Email subject.
        body: Email body.
        is_html: Whether body is HTML.

    Returns:
        dict with status, error.
    """
    if EMAIL_SERVICE_URL:
        try:
            payload = {
                "to": [to_email],
                "subject": subject,
                "from": DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,
                "send_all_at_once": True,
            }
            if is_html:
                payload["html_content"] = body
            else:
                payload["body"] = body
            resp = http_requests.post(
                f"{EMAIL_SERVICE_URL}/send-email-dont-reply",
                json=payload,
                timeout=10,
                proxies=PROXIES,
            )
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return {"status": "sent", "method": "email_service", "error": None}
        except Exception as svc_err:
            logger.warning("Email service failed (%s), trying SMTP", str(svc_err)[:120])

    if not SMTP_HOST:
        return {"status": "failed", "error": "No email transport available"}
    try:
        msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
        msg["Subject"] = subject
        msg["From"] = DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL
        msg["To"] = to_email
        msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "html" if is_html else "plain"))
        with smtplib.SMTP(SMTP_HOST, SMTP_PORT, timeout=15) as server:
            server.sendmail(DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL, [to_email], msg.as_string())
        return {"status": "sent", "method": "smtp", "error": None}
    except Exception as smtp_err:
        logger.error("SMTP failed: %s", smtp_err)
        return {"status": "failed", "error": str(smtp_err)}


def notify_name_mention(
    meeting_id: str,
    mention_text: str,
    question: str,
    response_given: str,
    user_email: str = "",
    slack_channel_id: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Send notifications when the user's name is mentioned in a meeting.

    Args:
        meeting_id: Meeting session ID.
        mention_text: The text surrounding the name mention.
        question: The extracted question/context.
        response_given: The auto-generated response.
        user_email: Email for notification.
        slack_channel_id: Slack channel for notification.

    Returns:
        dict with channels notified, error.
    """
    channels_sent = []
    errors = []

    formatted_msg = (
        f"*Your name was mentioned in meeting `{meeting_id}`*\n\n"
        f"> _{mention_text}_\n\n"
    )
    if question:
        formatted_msg += f"*Question/context:* {question}\n\n"
    if response_given:
        formatted_msg += f"*Auto-response given:* {response_given}\n"

    if slack_channel_id:
        result = send_slack_notification(slack_channel_id, formatted_msg)
        if result["status"] == "sent":
            channels_sent.append("slack")
        else:
            errors.append(f"slack: {result.get('error')}")

    if user_email:
        email_body = formatted_msg.replace("*", "").replace("_", "").replace(">", "")
        result = send_email_notification(
            user_email,
            f"Meeting Alert — Your name was mentioned ({meeting_id})",
            email_body,
        )
        if result["status"] == "sent":
            channels_sent.append("email")
        else:
            errors.append(f"email: {result.get('error')}")

    return {
        "status": "sent" if channels_sent else "failed",
        "channels": channels_sent,
        "error": "; ".join(errors) if errors else None,
    }

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/session_store.py | size=4260 | sha256=1ca3e3b9969e07b130692395902d8120c0891cfe85ec777a5385173d3ba5b1f3 =====
"""In-memory meeting session store."""
import threading
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any


@dataclass
class MeetingSession:
    meeting_id: str
    meet_link: str
    space_name: str = ""
    user_name: str = ""
    wake_name: str = ""
    user_email: str = ""
    slack_channel_id: str = ""
    status: str = "initializing"
    mode: str = ""
    context: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    transcript: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    name_mentions: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    started_at: datetime | None = None
    ended_at: datetime | None = None


_lock = threading.Lock()
_sessions: dict[str, MeetingSession] = {}
_pending_context: dict[str, str] = {}


def create_session(
    meet_link: str,
    user_name: str,
    wake_name: str,
    user_email: str = "",
    slack_channel_id: str = "",
) -> str:
    meeting_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    session = MeetingSession(
        meeting_id=meeting_id,
        meet_link=meet_link,
        user_name=user_name,
        wake_name=wake_name.lower(),
        user_email=user_email,
        slack_channel_id=slack_channel_id,
        started_at=datetime.now(),
    )
    with _lock:
        session.context.update(_pending_context)
        _sessions[meeting_id] = session
    return meeting_id


def get_session(meeting_id: str) -> MeetingSession | None:
    with _lock:
        return _sessions.get(meeting_id)


def get_active_session() -> MeetingSession | None:
    with _lock:
        for s in _sessions.values():
            if s.status in ("initializing", "joining", "active"):
                return s
    return None


def update_status(meeting_id: str, status: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.status = status
            if status == "ended":
                session.ended_at = datetime.now()


def update_mode(meeting_id: str, mode: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.mode = mode


def update_space_name(meeting_id: str, space_name: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.space_name = space_name


def update_wake_name(meeting_id: str, wake_name: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.wake_name = wake_name.lower()


def add_context(meeting_id: str, topic: str, data: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.context[topic] = data
        else:
            _pending_context[topic] = data


def store_pending_context(topic: str, data: str) -> None:
    with _lock:
        _pending_context[topic] = data


def get_context(meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        return dict(session.context) if session else dict(_pending_context)


def append_transcript(
    meeting_id: str, speaker: str, text: str, timestamp: str
) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.transcript.append(
                {"speaker": speaker, "text": text, "timestamp": timestamp}
            )


def get_transcript(meeting_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        return list(session.transcript) if session else []


def record_mention(
    meeting_id: str, timestamp: str, text: str, response: str
) -> None:
    with _lock:
        session = _sessions.get(meeting_id)
        if session:
            session.name_mentions.append(
                {"timestamp": timestamp, "text": text, "response": response}
            )


def get_all_sessions() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with _lock:
        return [
            {
                "meeting_id": s.meeting_id,
                "status": s.status,
                "mode": s.mode,
                "user_name": s.user_name,
                "started_at": str(s.started_at) if s.started_at else None,
            }
            for s in _sessions.values()
        ]

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/stt_streamer.py | size=5480 | sha256=45bde23ebfec14c8d125f6edcec8499696b9e2c9af0b4a21191b6899737da741 =====
"""Google Cloud Speech-to-Text streaming wrapper with auto-reconnection."""
import asyncio
import logging
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from google.cloud.speech_v2 import SpeechAsyncClient
from google.cloud.speech_v2.types import cloud_speech

from google.api_core.client_options import ClientOptions

from app.config import (
    GCP_PROJECT,
    GCP_REGION,
    STT_LANGUAGE_CODE,
    STT_MODEL,
    STT_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ,
    STT_STREAM_LIMIT_SECONDS,
    STT_MIN_SPEAKER_COUNT,
    STT_MAX_SPEAKER_COUNT,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

TranscriptCallback = Callable[[str, str, bool], Any]


class SttStreamer:
    """Streams audio to Cloud STT and fires a callback for each transcript segment.

    Handles the 5-minute stream limit by automatically reconnecting.
    """

    def __init__(self, on_transcript: TranscriptCallback):
        self._on_transcript = on_transcript
        self._audio_queue: asyncio.Queue[bytes | None] = asyncio.Queue()
        self._running = False
        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None
        self._client: SpeechAsyncClient | None = None
        self._recognizer = f"projects/{GCP_PROJECT}/locations/{GCP_REGION}/recognizers/_"

    async def start(self) -> None:
        self._running = True
        self._client = SpeechAsyncClient(
            client_options=ClientOptions(
                api_endpoint=f"{GCP_REGION}-speech.googleapis.com",
            ),
        )
        self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._stream_loop())
        logger.info("STT streamer started")

    async def stop(self) -> None:
        self._running = False
        await self._audio_queue.put(None)
        if self._task:
            self._task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        logger.info("STT streamer stopped")

    async def feed_audio(self, pcm_bytes: bytes) -> None:
        if self._running:
            await self._audio_queue.put(pcm_bytes)

    def _build_streaming_config(self) -> cloud_speech.StreamingRecognitionConfig:
        recognition_config = cloud_speech.RecognitionConfig(
            auto_decoding_config=cloud_speech.AutoDetectDecodingConfig(),
            language_codes=[STT_LANGUAGE_CODE],
            model=STT_MODEL,
            features=cloud_speech.RecognitionFeatures(
                enable_automatic_punctuation=True,
                diarization_config=cloud_speech.SpeakerDiarizationConfig(
                    min_speaker_count=STT_MIN_SPEAKER_COUNT,
                    max_speaker_count=STT_MAX_SPEAKER_COUNT,
                ),
            ),
        )
        return cloud_speech.StreamingRecognitionConfig(
            config=recognition_config,
            streaming_features=cloud_speech.StreamingRecognitionFeatures(
                interim_results=True,
            ),
        )

    async def _request_generator(
        self, stream_start: float
    ):
        config_request = cloud_speech.StreamingRecognizeRequest(
            recognizer=self._recognizer,
            streaming_config=self._build_streaming_config(),
        )
        yield config_request

        while self._running:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - stream_start
            if elapsed >= STT_STREAM_LIMIT_SECONDS:
                logger.info("STT stream limit approaching (%ds), reconnecting", int(elapsed))
                return

            try:
                chunk = await asyncio.wait_for(self._audio_queue.get(), timeout=1.0)
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                continue

            if chunk is None:
                return

            yield cloud_speech.StreamingRecognizeRequest(audio=chunk)

    async def _stream_loop(self) -> None:
        backoff = 1.0
        while self._running:
            stream_start = time.monotonic()
            try:
                responses = await self._client.streaming_recognize(
                    requests=self._request_generator(stream_start)
                )

                async for response in responses:
                    if not self._running:
                        break
                    for result in response.results:
                        if not result.alternatives:
                            continue
                        alt = result.alternatives[0]
                        speaker = "Speaker"
                        if alt.words:
                            last_word = alt.words[-1]
                            if hasattr(last_word, "speaker_label") and last_word.speaker_label:
                                speaker = f"Speaker {last_word.speaker_label}"
                        is_final = result.is_final
                        transcript_text = alt.transcript.strip()
                        if transcript_text:
                            try:
                                await asyncio.coroutine(lambda: None)() if False else None
                                self._on_transcript(speaker, transcript_text, is_final)
                            except Exception:
                                logger.exception("Transcript callback error")

                backoff = 1.0

            except Exception as exc:
                if not self._running:
                    break
                logger.warning("STT stream error: %s, reconnecting in %.1fs", exc, backoff)
                await asyncio.sleep(backoff)
                backoff = min(backoff * 2, 30.0)

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/tools.py | size=6438 | sha256=5e93cbabe566b3b8dd4c3c1db2b03a32f4a2a4b6541f3305ad49a0d9b487e4a4 =====
"""ADK tool functions for the Meeting AI Assistant."""
import asyncio
import logging
import re
from typing import Any

from app import session_store
from app.meeting_manager import get_manager
from app.notification_tools import (
    send_email_notification,
    send_slack_notification,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def feed_meeting_context(topic: str, context_data: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Feed context/data that the bot should know for the meeting.

    Call this before or during a meeting so the bot can use this
    information when auto-responding on your behalf.

    Args:
        topic: Short topic label (e.g. 'project_budget', 'timeline', 'team_size').
        context_data: The actual data or talking point (e.g. 'Budget is $2M for Q3').

    Returns:
        dict with status, topic, error.
    """
    try:
        active = session_store.get_active_session()
        if active:
            session_store.add_context(active.meeting_id, topic, context_data)
        else:
            session_store.store_pending_context(topic, context_data)
        return {"status": "stored", "topic": topic, "error": None}
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("feed_meeting_context failed: %s", exc)
        return {"status": "failed", "topic": topic, "error": str(exc)}


async def join_meeting(
    meet_link: str,
    user_name: str,
    wake_name: str,
    user_email: str = "",
    slack_channel_id: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Join a Google Meet as an AI assistant bot.

    The bot will transcribe the meeting in real-time, detect when
    your name is called, auto-respond using context you've provided,
    and notify you via Slack/email.

    Args:
        meet_link: Full Google Meet URL (e.g. https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij).
        user_name: Your display name (used in responses).
        wake_name: The name to listen for (e.g. your first name).
        user_email: Email for notifications when your name is mentioned.
        slack_channel_id: Slack channel ID for notifications.

    Returns:
        dict with meeting_id, status, mode, error.
    """
    try:
        if not re.search(r"meet\.google\.com/", meet_link):
            return {
                "meeting_id": None,
                "status": "failed",
                "error": "Invalid Meet link. Expected format: https://meet.google.com/xxx-xxxx-xxx",
            }

        manager = get_manager()
        result = await manager.join_meeting(
            meet_link=meet_link,
            user_name=user_name,
            wake_name=wake_name,
            user_email=user_email,
            slack_channel_id=slack_channel_id,
        )
        return result
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("join_meeting failed: %s", exc)
        return {"meeting_id": None, "status": "failed", "error": str(exc)}


async def leave_meeting(meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Leave an active meeting and stop transcription.

    Args:
        meeting_id: The meeting session ID returned by join_meeting.

    Returns:
        dict with status, transcript_length, name_mentions count, error.
    """
    try:
        manager = get_manager()
        result = await manager.leave_meeting(meeting_id)
        return result
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("leave_meeting failed: %s", exc)
        return {"status": "failed", "error": str(exc)}


def get_transcript(meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Get the current transcript of a meeting.

    Args:
        meeting_id: The meeting session ID.

    Returns:
        dict with transcript list, length, error.
    """
    try:
        manager = get_manager()
        return manager.get_transcript(meeting_id)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("get_transcript failed: %s", exc)
        return {"transcript": [], "length": 0, "error": str(exc)}


async def get_meeting_summary(meeting_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Generate a summary of the meeting with action items.

    Best called after the meeting has ended or has enough transcript data.

    Args:
        meeting_id: The meeting session ID.

    Returns:
        dict with summary text, action_items list, duration, error.
    """
    try:
        manager = get_manager()
        result = await manager.generate_summary(meeting_id)
        return result
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("get_meeting_summary failed: %s", exc)
        return {"summary": "", "action_items": [], "error": str(exc)}


def set_wake_name(meeting_id: str, wake_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Change the name the bot listens for in an active meeting.

    Args:
        meeting_id: The meeting session ID.
        wake_name: New name to listen for.

    Returns:
        dict with status, wake_name, error.
    """
    try:
        manager = get_manager()
        return manager.update_wake_name(meeting_id, wake_name)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("set_wake_name failed: %s", exc)
        return {"status": "failed", "error": str(exc)}


def send_meeting_notification(
    user_email: str,
    message: str,
    slack_channel_id: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Send a notification about the meeting to the user.

    Args:
        user_email: Recipient email address.
        message: Notification message.
        slack_channel_id: Optional Slack channel for notification.

    Returns:
        dict with status, channels sent, error.
    """
    try:
        channels = []
        errors = []

        if user_email:
            result = send_email_notification(
                user_email, "Meeting AI Assistant Notification", message
            )
            if result["status"] == "sent":
                channels.append("email")
            else:
                errors.append(f"email: {result.get('error')}")

        if slack_channel_id:
            result = send_slack_notification(slack_channel_id, message)
            if result["status"] == "sent":
                channels.append("slack")
            else:
                errors.append(f"slack: {result.get('error')}")

        return {
            "status": "sent" if channels else "failed",
            "channels": channels,
            "error": "; ".join(errors) if errors else None,
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("send_meeting_notification failed: %s", exc)
        return {"status": "failed", "channels": [], "error": str(exc)}

===== FILE: meetingassistant/app/tts_speaker.py | size=2504 | sha256=39121acddc68b5b31b2006f2de11da1827f3968ec0c459ebb1d0f1402d859a6f =====
"""Google Cloud Text-to-Speech wrapper."""
import logging
import struct

from google.cloud import texttospeech

from app.config import (
    TTS_LANGUAGE_CODE,
    TTS_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ,
    TTS_SPEAKING_RATE,
    TTS_VOICE_NAME,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_client: texttospeech.TextToSpeechClient | None = None


def _get_client() -> texttospeech.TextToSpeechClient:
    global _client
    if _client is None:
        _client = texttospeech.TextToSpeechClient()
    return _client


def synthesize_speech(text: str) -> bytes:
    """Convert text to raw PCM audio (LINEAR16, 48 kHz, mono).

    Args:
        text: The text to synthesize.

    Returns:
        Raw PCM bytes (16-bit signed little-endian).
    """
    client = _get_client()

    synthesis_input = texttospeech.SynthesisInput(text=text)
    voice = texttospeech.VoiceSelectionParams(
        language_code=TTS_LANGUAGE_CODE,
        name=TTS_VOICE_NAME,
    )
    audio_config = texttospeech.AudioConfig(
        audio_encoding=texttospeech.AudioEncoding.LINEAR16,
        sample_rate_hertz=TTS_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ,
        speaking_rate=TTS_SPEAKING_RATE,
    )

    response = client.synthesize_speech(
        input=synthesis_input, voice=voice, audio_config=audio_config
    )

    audio = response.audio_content
    if len(audio) > 44 and audio[:4] == b"RIFF":
        audio = audio[44:]

    logger.info("TTS synthesized %d bytes for %d chars", len(audio), len(text))
    return audio


def pcm_to_frames(pcm_bytes: bytes, frame_duration_ms: int = 20) -> list[bytes]:
    """Split raw PCM into fixed-duration frames for WebRTC.

    Args:
        pcm_bytes: Raw PCM audio (16-bit, 48kHz, mono).
        frame_duration_ms: Duration of each frame in milliseconds.

    Returns:
        List of PCM byte frames.
    """
    samples_per_frame = (TTS_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ * frame_duration_ms) // 1000
    bytes_per_frame = samples_per_frame * 2  # 16-bit = 2 bytes per sample
    frames = []
    for i in range(0, len(pcm_bytes), bytes_per_frame):
        frame = pcm_bytes[i : i + bytes_per_frame]
        if len(frame) < bytes_per_frame:
            frame += b"\x00" * (bytes_per_frame - len(frame))
        frames.append(frame)
    return frames


def generate_silence(duration_ms: int = 20) -> bytes:
    """Generate a silent PCM frame.

    Args:
        duration_ms: Duration in milliseconds.

    Returns:
        Silent PCM bytes.
    """
    samples = (TTS_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ * duration_ms) // 1000
    return b"\x00\x00" * samples

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Deploy Verizon Meeting AI Assistant to Vertex AI Agent Runtime."""

import os
import json
import vertexai
from vertexai import agent_engines

# ── Hardcoded constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
PROJECT_ID      = "vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0"
REGION          = "us-east4"
DISPLAY_NAME    = "verizon-meeting-assistant"
SERVICE_ACCOUNT = "sa-dev-voev-app-voevdo-0@vz-it-np-voev-dev-voevdo-0.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
CMEK_KEY_PATH   = "projects/vz-it-np-d0sv-vsadkms-0/locations/us-east4/keyRings/vz-it-np-kr-corp/cryptoKeys/vz-it-np-kms-voev"
STAGING_BUCKET  = "gs://voev-dev-voevdo-0-usmr-warehouse"

# Set to existing resource name to UPDATE instead of create.
AGENT_RESOURCE_NAME = ""

CONTAINER_ENV = {
    "SLACK_BOT_TOKEN":            "xoxb-your-token-here",
    "BQ_PROJECT":                 PROJECT_ID,
    "EMAIL_SERVICE_URL":          "https://email-service-vdsi.ebiz.verizon.com",
    "SMTP_HOST":                  "vzsmtp.verizon.com",
    "SMTP_PORT":                  "25",
    "DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL":         "do-not-reply@verizon.com",
    "CORP_PROXY_HOST":            "proxy.ebiz.verizon.com",
    "CORP_PROXY_PORT":            "80",
}


def get_requirements():
    return [
        "google-adk[gcp]==2.3.0",
        "opentelemetry-instrumentation-google-genai>=0.1.0,<1.0.0",
        "gcsfs>=2024.11.0",
        "google-cloud-logging>=3.12.0,<4.0.0",
        "google-cloud-aiplatform[agent-engines,evaluation]>=1.156.0",
        "google-cloud-speech>=2.27.0",
        "google-cloud-texttospeech>=2.17.0",
        "google-apps-meet>=0.1.0",
        "aiortc>=1.9.0",
        "av>=12.0.0",
        "numpy>=1.26.0",
        "protobuf>=6.31.1,<7.0.0",
        "slack-sdk>=3.27.0",
        "requests>=2.32.0",
        "playwright>=1.45.0",
        "nest-asyncio>=1.6.0",
        "python-dotenv>=1.0.0",
    ]


def deploy():
    from app.agent_runtime_app import agent_runtime

    print("Initializing Vertex AI...")
    print(f"  Project: {PROJECT_ID}")
    print(f"  Region : {REGION}")
    print(f"  CMEK   : {CMEK_KEY_PATH}")
    print(f"  Bucket : {STAGING_BUCKET}")

    vertexai.init(project=PROJECT_ID, location=REGION, staging_bucket=STAGING_BUCKET)

    existing = AGENT_RESOURCE_NAME.strip()
    if existing:
        print(f"\nUpdating existing agent: {existing}")
        print("(This can take 5-10 minutes)")
        remote = agent_engines.get(existing)
        remote = remote.update(
            agent_engine=agent_runtime,
            requirements=get_requirements(),
            extra_packages=["./app"],
            display_name=DISPLAY_NAME,
            description="Verizon Meeting AI Assistant — Joins Google Meet, transcribes, auto-responds, generates summaries",
            env_vars=CONTAINER_ENV,
        )
        print("\nAgent updated successfully!")
    else:
        print(f"\nCreating new agent '{DISPLAY_NAME}'...")
        print("(This can take 5-10 minutes)")
        remote = agent_engines.create(
            agent_engine=agent_runtime,
            requirements=get_requirements(),
            extra_packages=["./app"],
            display_name=DISPLAY_NAME,
            description="Verizon Meeting AI Assistant — Joins Google Meet, transcribes, auto-responds, generates summaries",
            encryption_spec={"kms_key_name": CMEK_KEY_PATH},
            service_account=SERVICE_ACCOUNT,
            env_vars=CONTAINER_ENV,
        )
        print("\nAgent created successfully!")

    print(f"\n  Resource name: {remote.resource_name}")
    print(f"  Display name : {remote.display_name}")
    print(f"\nCopy the resource name above into AGENT_RESOURCE_NAME for future updates.")

    with open("agent_runtime_metadata.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump({
            "resource_name": remote.resource_name,
            "display_name": remote.display_name,
            "project": PROJECT_ID,
            "region": REGION,
            "cmek_key": CMEK_KEY_PATH,
        }, f, indent=2)
    print("Metadata saved to agent_runtime_metadata.json")
    return remote


if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.environ["GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT"] = PROJECT_ID
    deploy()
